001....Page 



" kaman yanda na fad'a miki mijinki  aure zaiyi ba makuwa sai haka  yafaru dole sai kintashi tsaye " yace  mata, 
wani mummun boka  yake  mata magana yana lashe baki 😋



Kaman zatayi kuka tace " boka yanzun yazanyi kenan wallahi akan kishiya zanyi iya kome Plss taimaka min kusan wannan shine  aurensa na tara kenan"


y'ar budurwa dariya yayi yana Shafa wasu munanan kasunbarsa sannan yace " Salmah Kenan Ay wannan Karon bani zan miki aiki ba aljani d'an tsitsito zai miki aiki amma da shared'i hmmm bazaki iya ba "

"Haba boka har akwai abinda zai gagareni kome zanyi duk wahalar sa" tace kaman zata saka ihu,


           Murmushin mugunta yayi yana karewa boons dinta kallon makwat ya had'iye yawu kafin ya sassauta yace " aikin da za'a miki sai aljani ya kwanta dake kafin aikin yayu amma babu dole idan fa yayi miki  kuma  da shared'in  Zaki  daina  sallame asuba  da la'asar sannan  Zaki daina  wankar Janaba babu  sadaka kinji"

yayi magana kaman  bai damuwa ba amma a ransa kwad'ayinta yake kaman yayi Ya domin a bukace yake da ita. 


Zuba masa ido tayi kafin tayi murmushi tace " indai wannan ne mai sauki ne zaka samun ni dai bani k'aunar kishiya "

dariya yayi sannan yace "toh mushiga daga ciki ko"



mikewa tayi zuwa cikin D'akin me cike da shirgi da tarkace. 

Ay suna shiga yayi kanta kaman wani mayuwanci dakyar yabar tacire kayan jikinta Wanda dama su suka jawo hankalinsa hmm wuta wuta yake aika mata da kayan aikin  take jikinta ya amashi aikensa sai Mike take tana nishi  ,

Hmm boka kam babu bakin magana sai ihu da nishi itama tuntana ganewa har tabar gane kome domin boka Basauki sai da ya murzata yake kaman kayan  wanki  itama banda yatsina fuskanta take  tana toshe hancinta sabida warin da take fita daga  jikinsa Murray's yayi sonshi kafin yace " maza tashi kitafi karkiyi wanka Zaki samu ya dawo Kuma da bukatarki ya dawo don haka kiminka masa kanki toh duk abinda kika ce masa toh zance miki "

Dakyar take magana sabida  wahalar  Sarah's tace " 

Boka ya batun Uwarsan kenan domin ita kesashi kome fa"

.
Dariya yayi sannan yace " ba sai ta ganshi ba tukun ai wannan aikin har da manta uwa zan barshi amma karki yarda  ya had'u da Wanda yasani kuma kisa ido akan sa sosai muje na baki sauran magani "


haka suka fito daga d'akin ya had'a mata magani godiya tamasa sannan tafita bin k'ugunta yabi da kallon yana had'iye yawu Murmushi yayi yana Shafa kirjinsa. 



♡(∩o∩)♡

Dakyar tafito daga cikin sunkurun da tashiga wajen bokan tana zuwa tashiga motarta a guje tayi garin kaduna 


tafiyar awa guda tayi Sannan isa barrack din Kaduna amma na manyan sojuji gidansu shine a farko block A 1.


kana ganin gidan kasan na babban Soja ne masu tsaron gidan suna ganinta suka bud'e mata get din gidan tun kafin tayi parking ta hango motarsa ko d'ar bata jiba tafi tashiga ciki.


Tana shiga babu sallama ta kwalawa y'ar aikinta kira '" Blessing "

da gudu ta iso  tace " yes Mah"



Wani mugun kallon tabita dashi tace " oga  ya dawo ko kin kalle shi ko? 


Zubewa kasa tayi tace " I swear to god  mah banga tafityarsa ba plss kibarni anan"

Kaman zatayi kuka take magana, 

Yatsina fuska tayi sannan tace" ok jeki kawai "


haka tatashi jikinta a sanyanye tashiga kitchen tana mamaki Uwar d'akinta. 

Yana toilet yaji muryar ta da sauri ya gama yafito kafin  ya isa kofar har  tashigo wani irin kallo tamasa take ya susuce ya kama wani abu kaman wani sauna murmushi tayi tace " ya aiki "
"Alhamdulillah " yace, 

Yana mata wani shu'amar kallon irin Wanda ake cewa Zaki sani, 



tafiya take  kaman zatayi ba jikinta  har girgiza yake tsabagin kwarewa hmmm. 


Ai kasa hakuri yayi da jan ran datake masa jiki na rawa ya fisgota yace " Salmah horan Ya isa haka plss abani na hakkina Don Allah"


kaman wani abu arziki ta bashi dama yin yanda yaso da ita sai  da tabar shi yakunsa ninkaya tajanye Jikinta kaman zayi kuka yace " me haka  Salmah pls "ba musu tabashi had'nki 



sai da sukayi nisa tace "son nake kakira Ummin kace kafasa  Auren jibi "

ba musu yace" toh  Salmah" jikinsa  na rawa yakira Mmn Mahaifiyarsa sai da suka  gaisa kafin yace " Ummin nafasaa Auren "

dif yakashe wayar sannan suka  fad'a duniyar maji dadi 🤭

002....page 




Garin sakoto shiryar shirye bikin ake y'au saura kwana uku a fara bikin wata dattijiwa Wacce bata ta wucce shekara hamsin da biyar shige da fice take sabida bakin da Suke gidan masu aikin cin cin da sauran Abubuwar bikin wayarta dake hannunta fita tayi daga cikin jama'a ta Koma cikin gidan.


Duba wayar tayi  taga wanda ya kira sunan Khaleed ne ya fito akan screen din wayarta d'aukar kiran tayi ta Kara a kunne d'inta babu sallama yace " ummi na fasa auren"


ya kashe wayarsa zufa ne ya keto mata jikinta na rawa   ta zauna sharfa ta fashe da kuka  cikin kuka tace " 
...Innallillahi wa Inna illahi rajoun.... Wannan wani irin Rayuwa ne haka Khaleed ya jefa kansa ne yarinyar take nufi da ni ne dake falon akwai jikokinta y'an mata sune suka fita kiran Iyayensu " ikhlas ce budurwa a cikin su ita ce tafita kiran Mamanta suna fama da aikin tace " Ammah kizo fa Kaka tana kuka  "


tana cikin kwa6in cin cin kenan ikhlas ta fad'a mata da sauri ta cire ta nufi cikin gida tana shiga sauran kanenta suka biyo ta,


ganin yanda mahaifiyarsu take kuka yasa Fulani da Shuwa suka fara kuka wiwi domin sunsan kwana zance Kama na kullun ne ya fasa Auren 

cikin karfin hali Ammah tace " toh Ummi wannan fa ba abin kuka bane dole mu zama masu yarda da kaddara me kyau ko mara kyau amma kuka ba shine mafita ba addu'a ya kamata mu cigaba da masa ba kuka ba »« Shiru tayi sannan ta dubi kanenta da Suke kuka ta cigaba da cewa"

kuda zaku rarrashinta shine kuke taya ta kuka huumy lallai Kuna da dogon aiki a gaban ku bari nakira  Yaya Muktar mu ji me zai ce tun abokinsa ne ta yuwu ya yafi mu sanin abinda ya dace domin nayi imani da Allah ba za'a sake ba Yaya Khaleed aure ba indai a cikin wannan unguwar ne domin wannan shine Karo na hud'u kenan ana fasa Aurensa , "


wayarta ta d'auka ta kira abokin yayanta ta fad'a masa hall'o an sake fasa Aurensa da Nafeesa idan bashi da wani aikin yazo gidansu ga Ummi na kuka, 


*****
Can kaduna kuwa nin kaya Suke ta faman yi daga shi har ita Wai ma dan boka mata amma da yake ita d'in irin fitinanun matan nan ne sai da ya ture ta gefe kafin tabar shi ya huta wayarsa ta d'auka tace "

maza kira Iyayen yarinyar da zaka aura kace ka fasa ina jinka kuma daga y'au karna sake jin ka kira wani daga Sokoto kana jina ai "


haka ya gyad'a mata kai kiran Baban Nafeesa yayi sallama sai da suka gaisa sannan yace, " kayi hakuri Baba na fasa Auren Nafeesa duk abinda nayi na bar mata dukka "

kashe wayarsa yayi domin Baban Nafeesa hakuri yake bashi amma ina Uwarsa tana kansa hannunta ta mika masa ya bata wayar ba musu ya bata wani ruwa ta bashi fuska a tsuke tace " maza shanye kabani gorata "


jikinsa na rawa ya shanye ya bata kofinta wani bacci me karfi ne yayi gaba da shi kwanciya yayi.


Dariya take ba k'au K'autawa Shiru tayi kafin tace, " Hajiya Ummi Ke da Khaleed sai a lahira idan Allah yaso domin Khaleed nawa ne ni kad'ai domin ni aka yishi bye "


tunda daga wannan ranar Khaleed ya manta da kowa har Mahaifiyarsa. 

 ****

A can sokoto muktar ne a gaban Ummi yana bata hakuri da kuma mata alkawari aure nan ranar jumma'a babu fashi insha Allah...... 

Suna cikin wannan Hali Iyayen Nafeesa suka zo da nasu tashin hankalin dakyar Muktar ya shiga tsakaninsu ya kalli Iyayen Nafeesa yace " Baba Insha Allah jibi za'a yi kome kayi hakuri "


     zarro ido Baban Nafeesa yayi kafin yace " ni Musa Ay wallahi gara na ga gawar ta da na aura masa y'ata Allah ya rufa min asiri can da su gada Ke dai kitashi akan d'anki  domin ya had'u da shu'amar mace ne nabar ko lafiya "




kam a ce me magana ta bazu a unguwar an sake fasa aurens manjo Khaleed Hassan tambuwal a Karo na hud'u abinda yayi sanadin hawan jinin Ummi yayi mugun hawa da yake Muktar Dr ne shi ya duba ta har sai da  yaga bacci yayi gaba da ita sannan yayi musu sallama ta fita daga gidan. 


Zuciyarsa cike da tunani da yake shima fa yanzun ya kamata ya taimakawa Khaleed Kaman yanda shima ya taimaka  masa shekara  ashirin baya dama ce Allah ya bashi yanzun  ya dace......

003....page


Yanzun ya kama gidansu yatafi dama  unguwarsu d'aya da kuma gidansu kusa da juna yake amma shi yana doctor kwatas ne shi da iyalinsa. 


Gidansu yashiga da sallama. 
durkusawa yayi kasa ya gaishe da mahaifiyarsa yace " ina wuni  Inna mun same ki lafiya "

murmushi tayi cike da farin ciki da alfahari samun d'an d'aya tanker da dubu tace " Allah yayi maka albarka ya raya maka zuri'arka lafiya lau yasu Hauwa da yaran "

kujera yaja ya zauna kafin yace " suna nan Lafiya sai sai kawarki ce bata da lafiya amma da sauki ina Baba yake "


Shiru tayi kafin tace " hmmm ya tafi gidan malam balarabe akan maganar Khaleed ne "

ta karasa maganar kaman zatayi kuka sabida bakin ciki da abu da ya faru.



Gyara zama yayi sosai ta tattaro hankalinsa waje guda kafin yace " hmm dama akan maganar Khaleed ne ya kawo ni hmm Inna......"


sai yayi Shiru yana kallon kasa ya rasa ta ina zai fara mata maganar. 

dariya tayi sosai kafin tace " kana son ka taimaka masa amma karasa ta yanda zaka fara ba "

" .....Inna yanzun ya dace nima na taimaka masa Inna a cikin sun uku nan wacece tafi dacewa da shi a halin yanzun dole mu tashi tsaye akan al'amarin Khaleed Inna yayi min kome shekara ashirin baya me zai Hana muma musa halacci  domin yayi min na bashi d'aya daga cikin Y'ayana " yace mata, 



daga kofar waje suka ji ana cewa"

 wannan shine amintakar gaskiya 
ni Saleh na amince da wannan had'in da kayi Allah ya albarkacin su da zuri'ar d'ayyiba amma Wacece a cikin sun " injin malam Saleh kenan;

Mahaifin Muktar Shiru yayi kafin ya d'ago kai yace " Aishatu na bashi duniya da lahira halak malak na bashi ita sai sai bana son Ummi ta sani sai an d'aura Auren tukun ko me kuka gani "

zarro Idanun Inna tayi kafin tace " Malam k'asan me kuke fad'a haka matar Khaleed shaidaniya ce don Allah kubar wannan maganar  nan na roke mubar wannan zance "

      murmushi malam Saleh yayi sannan yace " Muktar tashi ka tafi gida Allah yayi maka albarka sai gobe tashi kitafi "

dariya yayi kafin ya tashi yace " toh sai anjima Ku"

inna kaman zata fasa ihu tana kallo Muktar ya fita Juyawa tayi ta kalle malam Saleh tace " kenan kun yanke hukumci ko toh duk abinda ya dawo  daga baya toh babu ruwana, "

dake tasan halin mijinta da d'anta ba zasu chaza ra'ayinsu akan abinda suka yi niyya shi yasa tayi Shiru cike da tsoran wannan had'in.

Yafita yayi ya shiga motar domin idan ba guduwa yayi toh Inna zata iya sasu a fasa hmmm akwai sauran rina a k'aba domin na Inna me sauki ne rikicin Hauwa kawai yake tunawa da haka yaja Motarsa.
****


Ba wani nisa tsakani sosai d'an tafiya kad'an yayi ya isa gidansa parking yayi Sannan ya kashe motar ya bud'e yafita shiga gidansa a da sallama  shiga.

y'an mata biyu ya samu a falo suna kallo Mikewa sukayi suka ce " Abba barka da dawowa yasu Kaka Suke "


kallonsu yayi kafin ya zauna a d'aya daga  cikin kujerun falon yace " suna nan lafiya Inna Mamanku take ? '"

Babba tace " suna kitchen ita da Ayusha suna had'a abincin dare "

kura musu ido yayi kaman me nazari kafin ya girgiza Kai yace " ku me ya hanaku shi aikin baku da wani aikin sai kallo ko hmm Allah ya shirye ku "


jikinsu ne yayi sanyi Mikewa yayi yabi hanyar kitchen din muryarsu yaji suna hirar har da dariya kaman wasu kawaye ,

gyara murya yayi yace " ana gulmata ko a sake daga farko "

y'ar matashiya yarinyar ce a kusa da Mamarta Wanda kana ganinsu k'asan akwai shakuwa sosai a tsakanisu kallonshi tayi tace " laaa Abba gulmar me zamuyi kawai Momy tana bani labarin takwara ce kawai shine nake dariya "

shima Murmushi yayi yana tuna Aishansa zuwa yayi ya rungume Momy ta baya  yasako kansa ta kafad'arsa. 

Ganin haka Ayusha tafita daga kitchen din tana dariya yarinya ce  da dude shekaranta Sha hud'u kenan sai dai kusan yanayin fatarta irin na zabiya ce Wato albino kenan Wanda yajawo bata gani sai da medical glass kuma Itace amarya major Khaleed. 


Bayan fitar Ayusha juyo ta yayi yana kallon fuskanta kafin yace " Hauwa fad'a min me sirrin ne "

harararsa tayi kafin tashiga neman bak'insa da yake ya gane abinda take nufi Kara tsayi yayi Mikewa tayi tajawo shi tasa hannunta a wuyarsa ta sakalosu kafin ta had'a bak'insa da nata tana zuba masa wasu fitinanun kiss shima hannunsa ya kai k'ugunta ya jawota sosai ya had'a ta da jikinsa sannan suka cigaba da kiss din kaman zasu cinye kansu🤭

004....page 



ganin abin nasa bana Kare bane yasa  ta jaye jikinta tayi kafin tace " kai Abbansu  yaran nan suna gida fa kuma ina Jin Kama a kwai magana a bakin ka da alamun yana da muhimmanci ne Koma me Allah yakai mu dare sai naji Koma me "

taka rasa maganar da dariya.


Shiru yayi yana kallonta  a ransa ay yana rikicin da za'ayi Ke hangowa kawai  hmm.


tare suka gama aikin abincin sannan suka kwashe zuwa kitchen shi da ita suna aikin suna hirar kiran sallahr magarib ne yasa shi yatafi d'akinsa itama da yaran suka tafi D'akinsu domin gidan d'akuna uku ne alwala yayi yatafi masalaci sallah. 

Suma sallahr sukayi sannan suka fito da alqur'aninsu suka shiga D'akin Mummy dinsu yayi musu Kari duk da Maryam ta sauke alqur'ani haka baisa ta daina tilawa ba.


Bayan Sun gama da 6angaren addinin books dinsu suka fito da shi inda tashiga duba musu Maryam kam dole ita Abbansu ne zai duba mata sabida ita y'ar jami'a ce tana matakin farko kenan.



Basu suka tashi ba sai da aka kira sallahr Isha'i sannan suka Koma D'akinsu sukayi sallahr suka fito cin abincin a lokacin Dr Muktar yashigo gida. 

Dukkansu dinne suka nufa domin cin abincin sai da suka yi addu'a kafin suka fara ci basu wani jima ba  Ayusha tace  ta koshe falo ta Koma ta kwanta kallon Hauwa yayi girgiza masa kai tayi abincin suka cigaba da ci kafin duk suka Mike alamun kowa ya koshe suma falon suka Koma inda suka cigaba  da hirar a man yake fad'a ma Hauwa  an fasa Auren Khaleed. 
"Hmm dama  wannan shaidaniyar zata bar Ayi ne yanda nake jin tana ikirarin ita ko a lahira bata ba kishiya sabida jahilci da hauka ni wallahi Ummi nake tausauyawa sabida duk tasaka ranta  aka Khaleed ya Kara aure " tace masa, 


Shiru yayi yana tunanin hanyar da zai bulo mata.



     A haka yayi ta tufka da da warwara har lokacin baccin su yayi kowa ya tafi d'akinsa  ya kwanta duk jikinsa  yayi sanyi har suka kwanta kallonta yake can yakira  sunanta  yace " Nifa  na na bawa  Khaleed auren Aishatu kuma jibi juma'a za'a d'aura Auren na Fad'a miki ne a matsayinki na Uwa kisan abinda Ke faruwa, "


wani irin hantsulawa tayi daga gadon ta fad'u kasa  kafin ta tashi jikinta na rawa  tace " rufa min asiri duniya  da  lahira karka jawo min ciwo   zuciya  babu gaira babu dalili Wacce  Aishatu wannan kalen hanjin Itace zaka had'a ta da wanca bamagujiyar don Allah kayi hakuri  da niyyarka inay laifin Maryam ko  Fatima sai y'ar aji hud'u scndry kalan  suje su hauka tamin yarinya ban isa ba wallahi "




"Hmmm ni kuma na gama magana na kenan kuma babu ruwana wanda zai hanani tunda Nayi niyyar haka " yace mata, 
sannan ya juya yayi kwanciyarsa. 


 "lallai ma wallahi  Muktar  abinda kake shirin aikatawa ba zai yu ba  wallahi  sai dai  idan zaka nuna min don bani na haifeta ba shine kawai " tace masa,

bata lokaci  da  ya sauka daga gadon ba sai huccinsa take ji a gabanta tsoro ne ya kamata jikinta  ya Kama rawa fincikota  yayi yace " daga  Iyayen kece  kawai xaki fad'a min magana  ba shanye  kin min na biyu kenan karki sake ba uku in ba haka  zan rabaki da ita  Aishatu naga karshe maganar  banza da kike Auren babu fashi "

Tureta  yayi sannan  ya Koma  ta kwanta ransa a baci a ransa  yace duk halinsu d'aya ne basu  da  tunani. 


ay Kuwa haushi taji tafita  daga  d'akin kofar da  bashi  yayi Laifi ba garram  aka sake shi ta Koma  d'akinta cike  da  bakin ciki da tashin hankali. 


****

Alhamdulillah jikin  Ummi da sauki domin har ta tashi tayi  sallahr magarib  da Isha'i ta d'anci abincin kad'an sannan  ta kwanta itama. 



****
A can  Kaduna  bayan  tashin  major Khaleed daga  bacci  ya manta  kome sai  Salmah kawai yake gani kuma aikin boka da yayi kenan....

*006...page*


A razana ta d'ago kai tana Kallonsa sai kuma ta sunkuyar da kanta hawaye na zuba a daga idanunta Muryarta tace "

...Abba da kai da kaya duk mallakar wuya ce nina amince duk abinda akayi indai bazai shafi karatuna ba toh Abba kayi  yanda duk ya dace "


wani farin ciki ne ya rufe shi kallonta yake yarinyar karama sai tarin hankalin Shiru yayi aljuhun Motarsa yasa hannunsa ya ciro tissue ya bata. 


Karba tayi tashare fuskanta bai sake magana ba sai da suka isa makarantarsu har zata fita yace" Aishatu kin amince ko ki min alkawari bazaki bijire min umarnina ba"

gyad'a kanta tayi sannan tace " Abba wallahi bazan bijire maka ba akan umarninka nayi maka alkwarin haka "

murmushi yayi kafin yace " muje na nawa malame ku bayani rashin zuwanki akan lokaci domin Ba'a a dukan amarya "

rufe fuskanta tayi sannan suka fita zuwa cikin Fomw da ke cikin garin sokoto har office din principal ya kaita sannan ya musu bayani dalilin later d'inta daga nan suka yi sallama ya tafiti itama class ta wucce jikinta a mace koda tashiga tasamu har anyi darasin farko na biyu tasamu bayan anyi tara kifa kanta tayi da kujeran bata san yanayin da take ciki ba na farko ita ba bakin ciki ba sannan ba farin ciki ba tayi nisa cikin tunani taje an dafata Tana d'agowa taga Kawarta Anisah kallon Anisah tayi sannan ajiyar zuciya kakaro Murmushin dole tayi dan bata son kowa yasan halin da take ciki tace " .....
neesah ya kike da fatan dai kome lafiya ya Ya zaid dinki. "

gyara tsayuwa Neesah tayi sannan ta Kare mata kallon kafin tace " Ayusha kina boye min damuwarki ni kuma bana boye miki tawa amma  ba damuwa yayi kyau "

shiru Ayusha tayi kafin dabara ya fadu mata murmushi tayi sannan tace " dadi na dake d'aukar dumi ni wallahi bani damuwa sai ciwon cikin da yasani agaba kinji abinda Ke damuna."


D'aga kafad'arta tayi sannan tace " ba damuwa in yayi wari maji "


hirar suka shiga yi Wanda ya taimaka mata wajen rage tunanin da take  dawa Abba zai had'a ta dashi. 

****
Komawa gida yayi ya Shirya zuwa asibiti sai da yafito Hauwa tasha gabansa tana girgiza  tsabagin bala'i cike da tsiwa  tace " abinda kika shirin yi bazai taba yuwa ba wallahi ina gani da idanuna ana    Atika y'ar wajen kawunsa aka had'a Shi da ita amma ko Wata uku Ba'ayi ba yarinyar ta mutu bayan tayi fama da zubar jini daga gabanta, aka sake bashi Nana y'ar gidan Liman haukatata akayi ga yahanasu itama mutuwa tayi har mata biyar uku mutuwa biyu hauka tubaran a haka tasa ya fatali da aure har hud'u a ce Aishatu Itace ta goma wallahi bazata sabu ba ayye gara tun wuri ka hakura "

tana kai aya ana kiransa  a waya abinda  yaji yace  shine  yasake gigitata tasa hannunta akai tasake kuka wiwi kaman yarinyar karama fita yayi yabarta amma yana jin zafin kukan da take zuwa yayi yakifa kansa a sitiyar Motarsa yana jin zafi har zai fita sai yafita daga motar yashiga ganin inda yabarta anan take  yasa  yayi kokarin d'agata amma fir tak'i ganin zata bata masa lkc yafita  ya barta ta cigaba  da kuka.

Dakyar takai kanta  d'akinta ga ciwon kan dake damunta 
magani tasha  takwanta bacci yayi gaba  da ita. 

****
Asibiti  yafara zuwa daga nan ya fita wajen wani Kakan Khaleed Wanda  yake tambuwal dake  mutumin malame ne bayan  ya yayi xaman jiran sabida yana  da bak'i sai da suka fita sannan yashiga tsohon yana  ganinsa  ya Mike yana  murmushi sannan "
yace Barka  da zuwa  Muktar ya hanya ya gida da iyalanka"

"lafiyansu  lau basu San zanzo ba " yace masa"


Shiru yayi sannan yace " Alhaji magana  akan abokinka ne har yanzun fa abin na matarsa gaba gaba  yake shaidaniya ce bata san Allah na nan ba"

dariya tsohon yayi sosai kafin yace " duk tsiyarta lokaci ne amma  ya mukayi da kai shekara hud'u da suka wucce na fad'a maka daga gidanka magani Cutar Khaleed zai fito rayiwarsa a yanzun tana  hannun Allah yana wajen Aishatu Itace zata iya raba shi d shu'umar matarsa ta wucce yanda aka tunani dole sai ka aura masa domin ba karami aikin tayi akansa ba a yanzun ita Ke sarafa tunaninsa mukuma  anan zama dole mu taya shi da addu'a domin ita Yafi bukata awaje mu. "


"Eh haka ne Alhaji gobe za'a d'aura Auren amma  zamu batar sabida karatunta Matsala d'aya nake fuskanta daga  wajen mai d'akina tak'i yarda da maganar Auren duk tashiga damuwa"

tsoho yace " dole ta damu domin duk cikin yaranku babu Wacce take so sama  da Aishatu kuma son marigayi ya shafi Aishatu kuma Ay tamaka halacci sosai kawai karabu da ita kayi abinda  ya dace domin nima jiya da tsofa na Sahura tasani Wai sai na sake ta kaji fa su tunaninsu duk d'aya ne basu da ban banci haka suke "
🤣

tattaunawa suka cigaba  dayi. 

****
Sha biyu dai dai kaman  mafarki taji muryarta tana mata magana gashi tana son tashi amma ya gaza haka tacigaba  dajin muryarta da bazata manta  da shi ba tace " yakike son Hana taimako bayan Ke ma'abociyar taimako ne karki Hana shi abinda yayi niyya ana sake baki amanrta don Allah ki taya shi aikin lada mana sannan kibata kwarin gwiwar akan haka ko ruhina za same salama a mankwancina kinuna mata zama  da Kishiya Jahadine aikine babbaaaaaaaaa"

Tashin tayi a firgice tana salati Aisha Indo muryarta taji agogon d'akin ta duba Sha biyu da minti Sha biyar zufa  ta sharbe tana tuna maganar da taji tashi tayi  shiga  kitchen ta d'aura  girkin ranar tana juya abinda  taji, tana cikin aikin  tajiyo muryar mahaifinta da sauri tafita ta bud'e masa  kofa  sannan  ta gaishe shi amma a madadin  ya amsa ruwan fad'a  ya sauke mata Sosaii kafin ya Koma  nasiha sannan  yayi mata sallama  yatafi. 




Bataan tafityarsa  ta Koma  ta had'a  aikinta  karfe  uku xata  aikin sai tara zata dawo dan hk  kiransa  tayi  da yaga nmbta kaman  bazai d'auka sannan  ya d'auka a k'asan  makoshi yayi magana yace " lafiya ina tuki ne "

Shiru  tayi kafin tace " dama zani aiki uku ne kuma sai hud'u Suke tashi ko zaka dauko sune"

"OK" yace mata,

Bayan ta gama ta shirya zuwa d'akinsa tana shiga  tasame shi yana ta lissafi tunda tayi sallama ya amsa yacigaba  da abinda yake domin sun Saba haka duk lkc da sa6ani  yashiga tsakaninsu idan Itace mai Laifi baya fushi lkc da ake rikicin sai tasauko yake hawa nashi fushi gashi tasan logonsa tasan yanda zata sauko da shi😝

  _warewa nayi domin sunyi nisa da duniyar mu sun fad'a nahiyar water melon bance kome ba ban fad'a kome ba_🤣🙈😝



Bayan awa guda suka dawo dai dai nan suka shiga wanka da suka fito ne suka zauna lissafin abinda ake bukata kayan d'akin amarya kenan can dai tace " Ya Muktar me yasa zaka had'a  Aishatu  da Khaleed amma idan ranka ya baci kayi hakuri bana son ranka yasake baci "


murmusawa yayi kafin ya gyara zamansa sannan yace " bari na fad'a miki hujjata na aurawa Khaleed Aishatu duk da k'insan wasu daga ciki amma wasu baki sani ba shekaru ashirin baya da suka wucce "

Asalin su Muktar da Khaleed sun fito ne daga Tambuwal amma mazauna garin sokoto Iyayen Khaleed suna da rufe asiri domin Alhaji Sagir tambuwal ya rike mukaimai da dama kafin ya rasu Alhaji Sagir yana da mata  d'aya  da yara  hud'u Khaleed ne Babba sai Shuwa sai Fulani da karama 
Shuwa  sunanta Safeena Fulani kuma Sakeena Karama ita kuma Khadeeja sunan Maman Alhaji Sagir kenan Hajiya Aisha Itace mahaifiyarsu  Khaleed suna kiranta da ummi.




Sa6anin gidansu Muktar Iyayensa basu da karfe amma  akwai  zuciyar neman nakai Malam  Saleh shine  Mahaifin Muktar Inna mero Itace  mahaifiyarsa  shi suka  Haifa malam  Saleh  masinja ne a ma'aikanta gona yana  da burin  Muktar  yayi Karatun boko Dan haka  duk Wata yana d'aukar  albashi zai raba  gida biyu rabi  nasu rabi na karatun Muktar. 


Muktar  da Khaleed  sun fara abotane tun wani fad'a  da sukayi  da juna  sai akayi  dace  karfinsu yazo d'aya don  haka  ba halin  wani  yasa wani kuka  toh tun daga  ranar  Shikenan tun  Iyayen  basu shiga  hidimar  juna  sai  gashi  sabida  abota  Iyayensu  har  sun k'ula  zumunci mai karfi gaske.


Alhaji Sagir babba burinsa  Khaleed ya zama  likita sai dai rashin sa'a  Sam Khaleed  baya  kaunar karatun likitanci abinda  yafi so shine hidimar soja tun yana  karami har suka shiga  scndry.

Muktar kuwa burinsa bai wuce  ya ganshi a matsayin likita ba har suka  gama scndry  kowa da irin  nashi burin. 

****
Bayan Sun gama ne Alhaji  ya samawa  Khaleed scholarship zuwa Cairo fir Khaleed  yace  baya so da Muktar  suka kama da sun dace shi Soja  yake.

A can gidan su Muktar  kuwa  jin kud'i shiga  jami'a  yasa Malam Saleh  yabashi hakuri ya nemi wani makarantar zayi kokari had'a kud'i  hannunsa dake Muktar yana  da hankali sai bai damu ba domin baya son saka Iyayensa a damuwa *****
Bayan kwana biyu alhaji Sagir  da kansa  yazo neman Muktar  yashirya yasama masa scholarship murna awajen su Ba'a cewa kome sai godiya kawai Alhaji Sagir ya fad'a  musu  yanda  sukayi  da Khaleed addu'a  suka musu. 


Bayan sati biyu suka  gama  shirin tafiya Khaleed  military school Muktar Cairo haka suka  rabu da juna suna kuka. 


****
Bayan shekara uku Muktar yazo Hutu  Dan daman shekara Shida zayi abisa tsarin k'asarmu Egypt ana bawa dalibai masu aure gida kaman Misali idan mutum yana  da mata toh suna bashi gida. 

Tunda Muktar  yasam kansa Allah  ya jarabe shi da lalura Wanda yajawo sau biyu ana masa  aiki a mararsa  daga  karshe  suka  bashi gida  Sukace yayi aure yazo  da matansa  Itace  maganin ciwonsa. 


lokacin da yazo  yarasa Wanda zai fad'awa sai aka yi dace Khaleed  yazo hutu  nan  ya fad'a masa kome Ay  Khaleed  baiyi kasa  gwiwa ba ta fad'awa  Umminsa ita kuma  ta fad'awa Alhaji Sagir shi yasame  malam Saleh  da maganar malam baiki ba y'ar wan ado Wato  Hauwa Inna mero  aka had'a su aure.



kafin  ya Koma  makaranta akayi bikin lkc da aka  kawo  amarya  d'akin  Inna aka  sakata Inna  tace idan  sun tafi  can sayi amarcin 


su Muktar  anci Alwashin wannan  ranar  duk  da yasan  yarinyar kafin  ya tafi  sake domin kafin Auren sau biyu  suna  zuwa wajenta HHauwa tana da hankali  gashi  ta gama  scndry Tana  da kyau  d'an  dai dai  ba  Laifi.


**** 
Watansa guda ta d'auketa  suka  Koma  inda  suka  dibi kwana  uku  suna  hucce  gajiya  kafin nan ango  ya goge amarya  son  ranshi  kaman  bazai barta ba. 

Nan  yashiga  bata  kulawa  na musaman. 

tun tana  kuka  na rashin  sabo  har ta zama  y'ar gari  kullun  idan  ta dawo  daga  makaranta  fita  yawo  Suke  Karba  takardun ta yayi  ya Nema  mata skull  of nursing da take yana samun kud'in da ake  warewa  dalibai. 

***
Shekaransu  guda  t haifi  Maryam  .

ba wani  hidima  sukayi  ba. kuma  babu  wani  me kuka da ita  Hauwa  da Maryam sau Muktar Ay Maryam  na da shekara  d'aya  Hauwa  tasake samun  wani  cikin. 
lafiya lau ta haife  y'arta  mace  aka samata  Fatima.

*009....page*



Bayan anyi suna ta cigaba da zuwa makaranta haka shima karatu yake baji ba gani. 


Fatima nada shekara guda suka gama lkc Maryam na da Shekara biyu kenan suka dawo gida abin mamaki shine yanda suka samun an sakawa gidansu Muktar fasali zubawa gidan ido yayi yana tunanin hanyar da zai tambaye wanda yayi sannan aikin. 

ginin zamani akawa gidan me kyau har da part d'insa sannan aka Zuba kome a cikin gidan. 

Gidansu Khaleed yaje ya gaida su Ummi anan yake tambayanta waye da wannan aikin Shiru cike da jimami tayi kafin tace " Ay daminar da ta wucce anyi ruwa sosai toh yasa Innarku bata nan tafiya bayan an gama ruwan kawai rushewar gidan aka ganin duk hankalin kowa ya tashi dan har muna tunanin ko tana cikin gidan sai daga baya aka gane bata ciki toh ina jimamin kenan sai ga abokinka yazo shine yace Inna tashigo gidan nan ta zauna ba yanda bayi da malam Bai amma yaki karshe har sai da Alhaji yasa baki kafin ya dawo nan toh a cikin wata biyu aka gyara gidan ban fad'a maka d'an kome ba sai kasani dole ne amma aikin abokinka ne kome daka gani "

D'ago kai yayi idanunsa yana tsiyayar da Kwalla yace " Ummi Khaleed ya gama min kome a rayuwarta bani da abinda zan iya biyanshi da shi ba sai addu'a Allah yasa yabiya shi da gidan aljanna  yana ena yanzun  ena suka tura shi "


" Yana can wai sun tura shi yankin bakasi ana yake aikin amma ya kusan zuwa" tace masa, 


Sun dade suna hirar kafin ya mata sallama yatafi gidansu. 

****

Wasa wasa sai da Khaleed yayi Wata Shida kafin ya dawo Ba karami farin ciki sukayi da Dawowar junansu ba. 

Lokacin da Khaleed yaga Maryam da Fatima dariya yayi sannan yace " kai abokina gaskiya nima nakusan aure dubi yaran nan sai kace wasu black American gaskiya aure zanyi "


Murmushin Muktar yayi kafin yace " ba damuwa Allah ya kaimu lkc "

kallonsa Khaleed yayi na d'an wani lkc kafin yace " ka Fara aiki ne "

girgiza Kai yayi sannan yace " har yanzun ina kan neman aikin......! Khaleed nagode sosai da abinda kama iyayena Allah yabarmu tare bansan da wani kalma zan maka Godiya ba amma Allah yasaka da alkhairi" 

"haba dai Muktar Iyayenka nima iyayena ne abinda naga  ya dace nayi kabar gode min maganar aikinka kawo min takardun Kai da Hauwa zan sama muku Insha Allah don dai Abba  bashi da lafiya ne ai da tuntuni kafara aiki ay"


takardunsu ya kawo wa Khaleed da kansa yaje har sakatiriya gwanati  tarayya dake tasan kantumar karamar hukumun sokoto Rabi''u tambuwal alhj Sagir ne yasama sa aikin. 

Rabi'u yayi ma Khaleed alkawarin Insha Allah za'a tura  takardunsu zuwa ma'aikinta kiwon lafiya. 



Sati biyu sai gashi ana neman Muktar da Hauwa suje interview tare suka je aka musu kuma Masha Allah kome yayi kyau domin an d'auke su aikin a babban asibitin sokoto. 

          Ranar da suka karbe sakamokon su basu iya magana ba har sai da suka iso gidan kafin Muktar yabi kan kowa Khaleed ya Fara runguma yana kuka sukansu Iyayensu sunsan irin son da Khaleed yakewa Muktar suma tayashi kuka Suke  sabida ba kasafai irin wannan abotar ba. 


Bubuga bayan Muktar Khaleed yake yana dariya domin shima karfi hali yayi kawai irin na mazan fama😜


"isa nifa nayi ne  dan Allah ba don kome ba...! Dama  nazo na fad'a muku ne yanzu haka antura ni Liberia kuma y'au suka ce ba tafi don Allah ga iyayena shekara biyu zanyi na dawo don Allah ka kula min da su kasa min ido akan Shuwa da Fulani karama kan bata da wani way'o ga Abba bashi da lafiya kutaimaka min ka kula min da su. " yace masa, 


rike hannun juna sukayi Muktar yace "wallahi zan rike maka ahalinka har ka dawo karka zan zama kai har sai ranar da ka dawo Insha Allah "

cikin kwarin gwiwa Khaleed yace " muje  ina jirana  a waje  nariga nayi sallama  da su Ummi" rakoshi Muktar yayi ina fitowa ya ga har motar sojoji Sun zo daukarsa  haka yashiga suka tafi da shi .



Tafiyar Khaleed  da kwana uku Allah yayiwa Alhaji Sagir rasuwa domin yayi fama  da jinya. 

Muktar ne kansa da sauran y'anuwansu Khaleed  na tambuwal. 

Rasuwar  ta jijiga Muktar bashi ba har jama'an  anguwar haka  aka shirya shi aka kaishi gidansa nagaskiya *(Allah kajinkan Muslumin da suka rigamu gidan Gaskiya)* Amin.


Bayan kwana bakwai kowa  ya watse sai su Ummi da wasu daga  cikin y'an uwansu  na. jikin 



****
Bayan shekara d'aya da fara aikin Muktar  da Hauwa kaman  kullum  tare ake  had'a su aikin har tsokanan su mutane Suke Masoyan karshe zamani domin tare Suke kome idan suna  aiki  zaka ji Muktar  yace baby bani  kaza baby yi min kaza aikinsa da kenan. 



Tohma haka suna ciki  aiki aka shigo da wani mutum da y'ar budurwa yarinya tana kuka shi mutumin yana sume bai san wanda Ke kansa ma ba. 

Tausayin yarinyar ne ya Kama Hauwa itama tashiga  kuka jawo yarinyar tayi tana rarrashinta har tayi Shiru Muktar akansa. 

Gefe guda Hauwa take tambyar yarinyar sunanta domin kalar fatar yarinyar ya burgeta zabiya ce har gashin giranta fari ne  Shiru yarinyar tayi kafin tace " sunana Aisha Indo "




Wayo tun safe nake abu d'aya har yanzun ban gama ba

*010....page*


Shiru tayi tana kallon Aisha Indo wani abu guda da ta lura da shi shine kaman Aisha Indo bata gani sake kallonta tayi a karo na uku tace " Indo me yasami Babanki haka "

      Share idanunta tayi kafin tace " ya kawo ni nan ne domin a duba min idanuna shine  wani yace  bazan sake gani ba shine bayan mun fito daga ciki Baffa na ya fad'i gashi daga Baffa bani da kowa. "
Ta karshe maganar cikin kuka sosai. 


tausayinta ne yarufe Hauwa duk ya cika zuciyarta kaman ta zauna  tataya ta kuka. 

         Har su Muktar suka gama babu wani cigaba domin ko numfashin bai dawo dai dai ba haka suka gaji suka zubawa sarautar Allah ido. 

           Jikinsu yayi sanyi Sosaii sabida basu ta6a ganin irin wannan ciwon ba kusan likitotti hud'u ne akansa amma duk sun kasa kome karshe oxygen aka saka masa ko zata taimakawa numfashin. 

   Duk abu ya dame su karshe dai haka suka fito daga d'akin. 

*××××*
Koda suka Koma gida Hauwa da tunanin Indo ta dawo gida duk tasa damuwa aranta har sai da Muktar ya tambayeta ko lafiya ce masa tayi babu kome. 

Washe gari da safe da ta tashi had'a abinci karyawa har su Indo tayi. 

Su Maryam gidan Inna mero take aikasu anan suke wuni, 

Idan taroki Inna tana so tasaka mata abinci rana zata turo a karba mata. 

Koda isa asibitin dama Muktar ya rigata zuwa d'akin su Indo tashiga ta mika mata abinci sannan ta tambaye ta  ya jikin Baffa ,


" da sauki " tace kawai, 

*××××*


Kusan sati guda Baffa Indo jikinsa dai toh sai yanda Allah yayi da shi domin babu wani cigaba, 

kullum sai Hauwa takai musu abinci sau uku a rana shakuwa ne yashiga tsakaninsu ita da Indo har da Muktar amma shi ba wani can ba tsakaninsa da Indo y'ar kauye shine magana dake shiga tsakaninsu. 


         Akwai wani lkc da Muktar yashigo duba Baffa toh sai aka kira shi da nmb kasar waje yana sakawa a kunne yaji muryar Khaleed sai da jikinsa ya d'auki rawa sabida kusan shekara guda kenan babu waya babu ayke tunda ta tafi gaisawa sukayi kafin ya tambaye shi ya jikin Abba. 

                 Shiru Muktar yayi sai da yasake tambayan shi inda inda yashiga masa Khaleed yace " abokina ka fito ka fad'a min meke faruwa karka boye min in fa muslumi ne karka damu duk kaddara da ta fad'u min hannun bibiyu zan amshe shi "


       Kaman baza ce kome ba sannan yace " kayi hakuri kaji amma ka kira Ummi zata fad'a maka kome am so sorry "

  kashi wayar gaba d'aya Muktar yayi sabida yasan halin Khaleed sarai sai ya iya kiransa ya tutsiye shi ina bazan iya fad'a maka wannan maganar ba.

Ashe ita Indo wayar da yake yana masifa bata mamaki taya mutum zata magana  shi kad'ai daga shi sai aljanar kararawa don haka tasa a ranta zata daina shige masa sabida gudun kar haukansa tayi tsamari. 

jiki dai sai fatan sauki amma babu wani cigaba. 

A haka suka rufa sati biyu kusan Hauwa Ke Hidima  da Aisha Indo Itace abinci kayan sawa sabulun wanka da wanki,
duk Hauwa ce bata  gajiya  domin Allah ya d'aura mata  son Aisha Indo. 

Su Muktar basu da aikin safe sai rana da safe Hauwa da Inna suka zo duba Aisha Indo bayan sun gaisa Indo ta juya kai ta kalle gefe guda can tace "  hmm kinsan wani abu kuwa Adda wallahi mutumin nan dake kiranki da sunan y'ar tsana bashi da lafiya gaskiya  kukai shi turu yana tare da aljanar kararawa sai yayi ta magana shi kad'ai abin tausayin "
Kwa6a fuska tayi alamun tana jin babu dadi abinda  Ke faruwa da Muktar dariya Hauwa tasaka  sannan tace " toh zamu Kai shi can turun kawai tunda bashi da lafiya "

haka suka  gama nata hirar  suka  tafi. 
A hanya Inna  ta kalli Hauwa tace " ga mace  har mace  babu Idanun Allah  yaba  Baffanta  lafiya  "'

"Amin " Hauwa tace, 
*××××*
Bayan kashi  wayar da Muktar yayi  jikin Khaleed ne yayi sanyi kiran Ummi bayan sun gaisa shiru  yayi kafin yace " Ummi  ta jikin Abba "

itama  Shiru tayi sannan  ya fara  masa  nasiha  har  ta fad'a masa  rasuwa  Abba  kuka yasaka kaman mace hakuri tayi ta bashi har yayi Shiru  yace" mun shigo wani garin  ne shi yasa har nakira ku amma  nanda  shekara me zuwa zan dawo domin za mu sake shiga wani kauye ne sai  nasake  kiranku "

"toh karka  damu Muktar  yana kula damu  Allah ya dawo  da Kai lafiya " tace masa ,

katse  wayar tayi sabida  tana jin tausayinsa. 

*××××*

Kusan kwana biyu  ana  fama  da zafi ne sosai  a sokoto  don haka Indo kasa bacci tayi inda tacire  riga  tayi  daurin kirji sai  fama  juye juye take sabida  bata  kaunar  zafi  ruwan da aka kawo musu haka ta juye shi a jikinta dakyar tabar na sallahr asuba. 

dake Muktar a asibiti  ya kwana  d'akin yashiga domin ya duba  jikin Baffanta yana  shiga  yasamu tayi wawan kwanciya duk zanin  ya kwaye sama cinyoyin duk suna waje rabin kirjinta  shima  a waje  ay  a 360 ya Koma  kuma  ya hana  kowa  shiga  sabida  alakanta  da Hauwa  shi yasa  ya hana  kowa  shiga. 


yana cikin  wannan  yanayin  ne  Hauwa  tazo  kallonta  yayi  kafin  ya tabe  baki  yace " wannar  yarinyar  sha shace  dubi  yanda  take  bacci  don  Allah  jeki  tashe ta sannan  ki duba min shi  ni zan tafi  sai yamma "

yana tafiya  tashiga  itama  yanda  ta ga  Indo  bata  ji dadi  ba tashin ta tayi  taje tayi sallahr. 

Hauwa  nacikin  duba  shi kenan  ya farka  da sunan  indo toh  tana  band'akin  Hauwa ce ta isa  wajensa  tace " Sannun  baba me kake  bukata  yanzu "

dakyar yake  magana  yace " ina ma zan  rayu  na nemawa  Aisha Indo  mijin da zai zauna  da ita  tsakaninsa  da Allah  da na bashi  amanrta amma babu "

   Cikin rawar  murya  tace " akwai  mutum  d'aya  kuma  na imani  zai riketa  ko y'au kake son  bashi  aurenta  da Amanart  a shirye  yake  ya amsa ka yarda  dani "

Shiru yayi kafin yace "toh  Allah yasaka  miki da alkhairi "
maganar  ma dakyar  yake duba shi  tayi  sannan  tafita  takira  Dr Faruq da sauran  Dr's d'in  suka  rufa  akansa.


Can dai suka  samu  kome  yayi  sai dai  har ya koma  bacci.

suku suku  ta wuni  a asibiti  aiki take  amma  zuciyarta tana hanata  sukuni  akan  abinda  take  shirin aikatawa karshe  karfafa  kanta  tayi bayan  ta tashi gidan  Iyayenta  ta nufa  tasami  Babanta  abinda  ya kawo wa ta fad'a masa  har da rokansa da yaje gidansu Muktar ya same Malam Saleh akan  haka  taimako ne zatayi..

Kallonta malam Isa yayi ma wasu dakika kafin yace " ta Yaro kyau take bata karko kije zan same malam Saleh amma baki gudu wani abu yazo ya faru daga baya bana son tashi hankalinki ne "


gyara zama tayi kafin sunkuyar da kanta sannan tace " Wallahi Baba bana tunanin kome sai alkhairi bana tunanin zata iya Cutar dani domin dukka shekarunta baza su wucce Sha biyar ba kuma kodan alkhairi da nake nufinta da shi Allah bazai bata Ikon Cutar dani ba Baba Yarinyar ce karama sosai kuyi hakuri da abinda zanyi karku hanani aikin alkhairi don Allah Baba "
    takarshe maganar kaman zatayi kuka, 
           Mahaifiyarta kallonta tsaya tana yi sabida mamaki da shakku, kafin tace "  Ya'ila k'insan abinda kike shirin aikatawa baki tsoran karta zo ta rabaki da mijinki da yaranki bazan hanaki ba amma ina tsoran karkirasa aurenki akan taimako tunda kinga haka kike so Shikenan Allah yasa mudace "

       murmushi tayi tana wasa da bakin hijab d'inta kanta a sunkuye, 
Numfashin malam isa yaja kafin yace " toh Bari damuwa kije zanyi magana da Malam Saleh d'in "
godiya tamusu sannan ta Mike tayi musu sallama tafita. 


     Asibiti ta koma tasamu Aisha Indo a raku6e a bakin kofar d'akin da Baffanta yake tana tsungune zuwa tayi ta dafa tace " mekike anan da baki shiga ba "
     juyawa tayi ta fashe da kuka har jikinta yana rawa tace " baffana yace min mutuwa zayi gashi banda kowa sai shi a garinmu kuwa koranmu akayi sabida wai ni ba y'ar aure bane sabida ba haka  yaran da aka haife su a aure Suke ba"
kuka tasake rushewa da shi sosai. 

    jikin Hauwa sanyi yayi a ranta tace sabon rikice kenan shiru yayi kafin tace " babu abinda zai same Baffa Insha Allah kinji maganar y'an garinku kimanta da shi ni ina tare dake kinji kO"

Malam isa yaje 
yasami Baban Muktar da magana karfafawa malam Isa gwiwa malam Saleh yayi sabida abinda  Hauwa tayi ta burge shi kuma ya amince da taimako da take da niyyar yi. 

Da dare kafin Muktar ya tafi asibitin yake zolayar Hauwa da batun haihuwa abinka da abokan wasa dariya tayi tace " tayu daga su  Fatima hala bazan sake haihuwa bane kaga sai ka kara aure ko "


   dariya yayi sosai kafin yace "waya ganki da kishiya hmm ba damuwa koke sai kinemo min ba shikenan ba an kashe boss kenan"

          " Da gaske kake ko da wasa "tace masa, 

    " toh da wasa nake miki ko kinga wasa ne a fuskana " yana magana yana mata murmushi, girgiza kai tayi kafin tace " hmm daman nasan wasa kake Shikenan " ta karsa magana a shagwabe har tana tature shi ita taji haushi, 

        rungumeta  yayi sosai yace " rufa min asiri ina ni ina mata har biyu wallahi Bazan iya ba mu bar zance haka domin ni wasa nake miki kawai "

     Da haka suka bar maganar amma ita ta kudiri a niyyar haka "

washi gari har asibiti su malam suka je da jagoranci Hauwa Sun samu Baffa yana cikin hayacinsa amma baya magana sosai sabida numfashinsa dake d'aukewa ya dawo wajensa Hauwa nufa cike da tausayinsa ta isa wajensa tace " sannu Baffa ga iyayena sun zo akan Aisha Indo ko zaka basu aurenta "

     Kud'i taciro a jakarta ta Mikewa malam Saleh. 
amsa yayi yana tausayinsu dukkansu ya isa wajen Baffa "  Sannun ko Allah ya baka lafiya munzo maganar auren ko zaka iya bamu da kanka sai mu Karo shaidu ko mu bari sai kaji sauki sai Ayi kome fa lafiya yafi ai "

    girgiza Kai yayi da kyar yace " nina baku ita kuyi yanda ya dace domin ba tashi zanyi ba don Allah kwaye ya riketa da amana ita kad'ai na haifa bani da kowa yanzu sai ita Koda cikin dangina bana tunanin zasu riketa amma ina jin ku zaku mata gata sosai "

duk jikinsu yayi sanyi Malam isa ne ya zama waliyi din Muktar Malam Saleh yayi na Aisha Indo ina shirin d'aura Auren bazata Dr Adam da Dr Sadeeq suka shigo fita Hauwa tayi tana share hawayen farin ciki ko nace akasin haka, 

     Abin mamaki ana d'aura auran Baffa Aisha yana cikawa abin ya girgiza su sosai Hauwa aka kira ta tafi da Aisha Indo Wacce bata san meke faruwa ba   kukan da Hauwa take boyewa yafito fili sabida tausayinta, 

  Shiru tayi can tace  " Maman Maryam kukan me kike kuma ina zaki kaini ina Baffana ni wajensa nake so bazani ba "

 
    Cikin kuka Hauwa tace " kiyi hakuri kinji Baffanki ya rasu kuma yanzun Ke matar aure ce karkiyi kuka da yawa yafi bukatar addu'arki kinji ko"

rawa jikinta yake har suka isa Dr's quarters neman kukan tayi ta rasa sai zare Idanun da suka kad'a sunyi jajur har wani d'aki Hauwa takai ta tace" zauna ina zuwa kinji "

wajen Muktar tashiga tasame shi yana aikin jin motsinta yasa  ya d'ago kai " lafiya kika dawo gida bayan lkc da zaki tashi bayi ba  " yace mata, 
kukan ta take boyewa ne  yafi tace " Baffa  Indo  ya rasu amma  kafin nan nayi maka karanbani nasa su Abba Sun nema maka auren Aisha Indo kayafe min abinda nayi maka tausayinta  da Sonka suka kaini ga aikata haka 

durkusawa tayi a gabansa  tana kuka me cin rai jikinta  har rawa yake sabida tsoran  abinda zai ce,



abinda yake  ya Bari  yazo  gabanta  ya mikar  da ita  ya share hawayen dake zuba  a idanunta da harshinsa  bak'insa  yasa ya had'a  da nata yashiga  aiki  mata  da wasu zafafan  kiss bai kyaleta ba sai da ya ga babu kukan kafin  ya barta  yace " da wani ne yayi min haka  wallahi  sake  matar zanyi  amma Ke bazan iya  zuba  miki  kasa  a Idanun ba bari naje  Jana'iza kar na rasa "


fita yayi  yabarta  a gidan  d'akin  Indo  takoma  tasame a  kwance  zazzabi  yarufe magani  ta bata  da abinci  dakyar taci tana kuka itama  kuka  Hauwa  take sabida  tausayinta, 



bayan Dawowar su daga Jana'izar bai bita kan Hauwa ba ko  Indo  a'a  aikin  ya tafi

Bayan wata uku da rasuwa Baffan Indo har  ta fara  manta  kome  tasaki  jiki  dake  kome  Hauwa  kamata  toh ta bangeren Muktar shi kallon  kwaila  yake  mata  har  yanzun  gata  y'ar kauye ba yanda  Hauwa  batayi ba akan  ya raba  musu  kwana kallon  d'aya  ya mata  kafin  yace " ina  raba inji me  a Jikinta dubeta  fa Haba Ayko Ke da muke tare k'insa  ni bana wasa bane  kawai karabu dani kawai  ina yin wani abu da ita  sai  amata  dinki kaman  kwarya "


   _fans Kuna jin Muktar  ko_


tun daga ranar  bata  sake  masa  magana  ba. 

*××××*

Rayuwa natafi  dasu ta kiwacce  fuska tazo musu suna amsarta wani girma  Aisha  tayi fatarta ta goge  tayi kyau duk wani  halittar jikinta  ya fito rasss ga mace  har mace sai dai  babu  Idanun  shekarunta  shabiyar  cur idan kaga kirjinta  zaka  Tsamaci takai shatara  a'a girman jikina  kawai gashi  bata son sa riga d'aurin  kirji take  abinta Muktar  yayi  fada  Hauwa  tayi fada ko tabari  kwana biyu  zata  cigaba akwai  ranar  da Muktar  yayi  mantuwa ya dawo  d'auka d'aga  ita sai fatima wasa  guje suke  dake  Maryam tana gidan  Inna mero kawai  ta danno  falo  lkc ita  fatima  tana kan kugera dai dai zaninta  ya fad'i kenan  daga  ita sai  under wear sai  pant kirjinta babu brzy kasa rufe idanunsa  yayi  a hankali  yake  takawa  lkc  har  ta d'aura zanin zuwa  yayi  ya d'auke ta  cak zuwa  d'akinsa Ay kuwa  ra ware muryar  zata  danna masa  ihu  toshe  bakin  yayi sannan  yace  " na hana ki  zama  ba riga  amma  baki ji Bari nayi maganinki gobe bazaki sake zama  ba riga  ba"


yana shiga d'akinsa  ya jefata  saman katifa  bita  yayi yashiga  rage  zafi ya manta  da Fatima  dake  wasa a falo hakuri  take  bashi  bai kulata ba  shi da zayi  haka ne dan ta daina  zama  ba riga  sai  ga Muktar har  ya fara  kirari iyakar  kasa da kasa  smooth nd soft skin d'inta  ne yabashi  gudunmawa Sannun  a hankali  har   ya cinma  nasara  akan bakuwa yanayin  da yashiga duk da yabita  nutse  bai  hana  taji zafi ba  domin  tasha  azaba  ihu  kuwa  da makota  nakusa  da sun kawo  mata  d'auki  sai  bayan  awa guda  ta barta  yana  hutuwa jawota  yayi  jikinsa  ba wai  dan kansa  yabarta  ba a'a  sabida  yanda  ta gala6eta yasa ya  barta.

Dubata   yafara  sannan  yaga  babu rauni  awajen wanka  yayi  yazo  ya had'a mata  itama  sannan  yakaita  ya  Sata  a cikin ruwan  wani marayan kara tasaka sau uku  yana sake  mata  ruwa  sannan  ya tambaye ta ta iya  wanka tsarki  gyad'a masa kai tayi fita  yayi  ya Koma  falo  har Fatima  tayi  bacci  .
Kitchi yashiga  ya had'a  mata  shayi  me kauri da ya shigo  ya ajiye  band'akin ya Koma  ta dauko  ya ya d'aura ta  a saman  gado  shaiyi yabata  tasha sannan  yabata  magani   sanan  yace " bari naje  na dawo  ko  ki kwanta  zamu dawo ni da Maman Maryam "
nan na gyad'a tayi wani al'amari mai girman  yake ji game da ita sone ko me haka  yake Juyawa  karshe  yabashi  son  wanda  yashige shi  lkc d'aya. 





A can asibiti  kaman  yayi fifike  yatashi  kunsa kowa  ya d'ago  yana  ciki  farin ciki a haka  har suka dawo gida tundaga  bakin  kofa suka jiyo kukanta  ita da FATIMA  da sauri  suka shigo  turss  Hauwa  tayi  jin muryar Aisha Indo  a d'akin sa  Juyawa  tayi  zata  Koma  ta d'auki  y'arta  da da  sauri  ya ruko  hannunta  yana  girgiza  kai  hankali  yace " abinda  yadace  nayi  banyi  kuskure ba amma  kiyi  hkr  idan  na xalumci ki "

Murmushin dole tayi kafin tace "a'a wallahi kawai na ga  yakama  kai  kaduba tane  shi yasa  na kuma  amma  kayi hkr na daina "

tare suka  shiga D'akin  taimaka  mata  tayi  har suka  shiga  band'akin  kid kuka  Aisha Indo  tasa  tace " kaii  Maman Maryam  Abba  maryam  mugu ne gabaki  d'aya  yayagani dubi  yanda  yasa  cinyana Suke  ciwo  gashi  mafutsarana... "

*012....page*





Kallon tausayi Hauwa ta mata domin tasan waye Dr Muktar ciki da waje kuma tasan shi akan yanayinsa mutum ne mabukaci amma yanda yabi da Aisha Indo ba sauki  "sannun ko kiyi hakuri wannan shine auren karki sake fad'an cewa shi mugune babu kyau kuma karki fad'awa kowa abinda yayi miki sirrinkune Ke dashi kinji anan da wani lkc zaki warke shiga ruwan zafi zaki ji dadin jikinki " 

         K'ollan dake idanunta ta share sannan tashiga ruwan runtsa idanunta tayi sabida zafin dake ratsata kuka tacigaba dayi abinda Hauwa ta fahimta Aisha Indo tana da raki sosai sau biyu tasata kafin tayi alwala tafito daga ciki shimfid'a mata praye mat Hauwa tayi sannan tasa yanda zatayi sallah dakyar take sallahr still tana kuka tana idarwa ta fara yarfe hannun ita fa cinyoyinta zasu fashe dan ciwo ga ko ena na jikinta yana ciwo sai da  yazo yayi mata allura sannan bayan ten minutes tayi bacci fita Hauwa tayi daga  d'akin sabida wani kishin dake dukanta shi yasa bata zauna a d'akin ba da taimakon Allah take samun sassauce .



Muktar Manya duk an sussuce akan yarinya karama me zata ji abinda  Zuciyarsa ta tuna masa kenan shafa kansa  yayi a fili  yace " Aishatu kin zame min wani yanke rayuwata Hauwa tazame min jigon rayuwata dukkanku biyu wasu shashi ne na rayuwata Ina sonku sosai Allah had'a min kanku "

       "Amin uban Y'ayana abin alfaharin mu muna sonka sosai "
Hauwa ce ta fad'i haka, 
Juyawa yayi ya runguminta yana shinshina wuyarta. 

Tun daga ranar suka d'auki kulawar duniya nan suka d'aura shi akan Aisha Indo kuma ko gaban waye Muktar baya shakan nuna musu kulawa na. 

A hankali Aisha Indo tasaba da rayuwa aure inda Hauwa ke nuna mata abinda  y dace da wanda bai dace ba Sam zamansu babu kishi mijinsu kawai suke kyautata masa duk inda hauwa zata shiga indai cikin y'an uwansu toh tare suke zuwa kowa ya ga irin zamansu saisu baka Sha'awa sabida had'in kai da suke sosai. 


*»"»"»*
Kwanaki na tafiya sati na zagayowa watanin suna shudewa haka suka kasance cikin farin ciki sakamako cikin da Aisha Indo Ke d'auke da shi daga Muktar har Hauwa Kai har Iyayensu babu wanda  baya farin ciki  sai  Aisha Indo kuma sun rasa meke damunta sai ramewa take ga ciki na girma. 


haka suka cigaba da kulawa da ita  har ta fara Anti netal.

Duk wani kulawa ana bata har gwaji na musaman Muktar yasa aka mata amma shiru kake ji. 
Babu alama ciwo a tare da ita haka suka hakura amma Dan kulawa tana samu. 



Abin duk ya dame Muktar da Hauwa zama sukayi har da ita Muktar yace " Indona fad'a min meke damunki ni da hauwa muna tare dake kinji fad'a min "
      girgiza masa kai tayi magana cikin sanyin murya tace " babu kome kawai dai ina jin tsoro ne akan wannan cikin na tashi bansan Innata ba ina gudu kar abin cikin shima  yataso bai sannan ni ba "

mikewa yaje kusa  da ita hauwa ma haka  suka ce" muna tare dake babu abinda zai same ki kinji ko y'ar kanwarmu "
     rungumeta sukayi suna dariya. 

*»"»"»*
hankali lkc Ke tafiya y'au suna  zaune ana hirar  dukkansu shiru Aisha Indo tayi  sabida kwad'ayin busar dankalin hausa da take ji. 

Hauwa ce ta lura da shirunta taba Muktar tayi ta nuna masa  Aisha Indo tana tunanin yanda zata ce musu zata ci dankalin hausa bayan ba cimar su bane taba ta da yayi shi  ya dawo da hankalinta jikinta ya firgirt tana kallon wajen kaman tana ganin sa, 

      " haba y'an mata gani a kusa dake tunanin me zaki kawai ce min iluv you kisha mamaki "

     karshe maganar yayi yana dariya sabida yansan bata son yana mata turanci hauwa ma dariya tayi tana jiran me zata ce. 

      Cuno baki tayi gaba tana kunkuni tana mita cewa " haka  kawai salon zagi a fakaice shine ake min turanci kome  yake oho ilimi  ya yahudu da nasara asaransa duniya "
murguda baki tayi sannan  ta mike  ta koma d'akinta ,
Suma Daria suka saka sannan suka bita d'akinta hauwa tace "Aunty Maryam me kike so  a kawo yanzun "

cikin sanyin murya tace " zanci dankalin hausa ce "

Muktar  hauwa ta kalla kad'a mata kai yayi  sannan  ya sakar mata murmushi kafin yafita  sayo dankalin a kasuwa yana  kawo hauwa ta dafa mata taci sosai kuwa. 

*»"»""»*

Y'anxun kan cikinta  yana da wata bakwai kenan dan haka duk wani abinda  ake bukata duk  yasaya ya ajiye babu abinda  bai saya ba. 


Kwance tashi asaransa  me rai cikin har yakai watan haihuwa  Wata ranar juma'a tafara jin ciwo amma  tayi shiru a hankali ciwo ke ta cinta har yakai bata iya ko magana  gashi cikin dare ne babu yanda zatayi dole ta hakura kafin wani daga cikinsu yazo dubeta  dake haka suke duk da suna raba kwana haka bai hana hauwa zuwa dubeta,


       yanzun ma haka, Kama  a mafarki  Indo tana kiranta da sauri ta sauka daga gadon tafita shima  ganin fitanta  yafi a tare suka  shigo d'akin  ganin tana  fizga alamun  sarkewar hakori  yasa Hauwa  tayi kanta  a guje  domin  har ta  fara  dukan cikinta  ga jini dake  zuwa  zuwa  yayi  ya d'auke cak  yafita  da ita  Hauwa bra d'auko  masa  key  din  motar sa  a guje  suka  fita  zuwa  asibiti domin tana  zubar da jini sosai,



      suna zuwa  aka  bashi  takarda  yasa  hannun  a shiga  da ita Cs yana  san hannun Wata  nurse  tafito  tace" sir  to haihu amma Kaman  akwai wani baby  domin har  hannunsa yafito"



    bata  gama maganar  ba Muktar  yashiga d'akin  dake  Sun  San  shima  likita  ne yana  shiga  ya ga  halin  da take ciki hankalinsa ya tashi  sosai  sunan  Hauwa  takira  dake  dama  tana kusa  da ita  amsawa  tayi  ,

sabida  wahalar  da tasha gashi  har  yanzu  tana  cikinta  tace " me na haifa  Mmn Maryam "


hauwa tace "
Mace "

Jikin Hauwa ne ya d'auki rawa da ganin yanda Dr Adam yafito kansa a sunkuye haka Dr Fa'iza da Dr Zainab duk suka fito jikinsu a mace ganin Ummin Ashiq da Hakabs Sun fito da Aisha Indo an rufe mata fuska yasa tayi baya zata fad'i da sauri chef nurse Maman Fareeda tariketa tana salati girgiza kai tayi tana kallon Muktar da yayi suman tsaye....

"please karku fad'a min kome ena take " tace musu tana zaro ido waje, 

Dr Adam zuwa yayi kusa da Muktar yayi yana kallonsa dafa kafad'arsa yayi ya ma rasa tacewa sabida tausayin abokin aikinsa. 

"kayi hakuri duk mai rai mamace ne Aishatu Allah yakarbe rayuwarta babu yanda muka iya muma yana kanmu am so sorry Allah yajinkata da rahama yasa can yafi mata nan "

                 hankalin ya takai har wajen gawarta ya bud'e yana kallonta fuskanta fesss da shi sai annuri yake hawaye ne suka sauko daga idanunsa Shiru yayi kafin yace " daman tafiya zaki kibarni Aishatu me yasa bazaki bari natafi ba sai Ke zaki rigani Allah yajinki mata Allah yasa mutuwa hutune a gareki Allah yasadaki da rahama.......... "
                 Kuka ne yakwace masa hauwa kam nema kukanta tayi tarasa sabida kid'ima da firgici tah kasa tsayuwa durkusawa tayi kasa dakyar ta mike wani irin juye mararta yayi sai jin ruwa me d'umi yana bin sawunta kuma da karfe wanda yaja ta fara ganin hajijiya  dakyar take takawa har gaban gawan tasa hannunta kenan zata dafa abinda aka  turo gawar kenan sai gani sukayi tatafi luuuüu,  cike da zafin nama ya runguminta yana jijigata yana kiran sunanta amma ina ta suma a wajen cikin dakin haihuwa aka shiga da ita sabida jinin dake zuba a Jikinta wanda suna kyautata zaton ciki ne da ita kuma da alamun ya zube, 


        d'aukart yayi ya d'aurata akan gadon da aka kawo sannan suka turata zuwa d'akin labour room. 

       Dr Zainab da Dr Fa'iza ne akanta sai nurse da suke taimaka musu. 

motar asibitin aka kawo sannan aka saka Aishatu zuwa gidan Iyayen Muktar  dake lokaci har an fara kiran sallahr farko na assalatu buga kofar suka yi suma sallama daga ciki malam Saleh yace " suwaye *?*"

      " Nine Baba "
Abinda  Muktar yace kenan, 
       ay jin muryar Muktar ya sa malam Saleh bud'e kofar jikinsa shima rawa ya d'auka yace " lafia da asuban fari  *?* ina iyalinka suke *?* meke faruwa *?* " duk a lkc guda ta watso masa tambayoyin haka, 

 ganin ba Muktar kad'ai bane har da wasu a bayansa ga motar asibiti me d'aukar hawa yasa ya karasa da y'ar touchligt dinsa  ta haska bayan motar ganin mutum a kwance  yasa  ya bud'e motar ya shiga sannan  ya bud'e fuskanta ya haska "Innallillahi.........." zaman da6asss yayi a ciki motar wani gume yana karyo masa a hankali ya shi yafita ya ga har yanzun Muktar yana tsaye inda  ya barshi shi da abokansa karasawa yayi yana kallon Muktar cike da tausayin d'an nasa yace " Muhammad kayi hakuri ko ku d'auko gawar a mata  wanka sai a sanar a masalaci bayan sallahr asuba Ayi jana'izarta "

       "toh " suka ce,


     gawar suka fito da ita sannan suka shiga da ita cikin gida malam Saleh yana shiga  ya fad'awa Inna mero kuka ta fashe da  shi sosai. 

     Gidansu Khaleed malam  yaje  ya fad'a ma Ummi halin da ake ciki dama  tayi sallahr taka'atul fijri sallahr nafila kafin sallahr asuba.  
Tare suka fito da su Shuwa da Fulani suna shiga  suka same  Inna tana kuka hakuri da banbaki Ummi tayi ta mata  sannan suka had'a kayan wanka sai da suka mata sannan  Inna tafito da wani farin  yadi irin wanda ake  saka gawa sutura tana zub da hawaye tace " ashe Ke za'a rufe da shi yanda  kika ce a ajiye miki zai yi amfani ashe naki ne " ta karshe  maganar tana kuka, 

       haka suka  had'a ta sannan suka  d'auki Alqur'ani suka fara  karantawa har aka  shiga sallahr asuba  suma suka ajiye  sukayi  sallahr bayan sun idar suka  cigaba  da karatun har aka zo fitar da ita sai  lkc mutuwar  Alhaji Sagir ya dawowa  Ummi sabo fil itama  ta zubda hawaye  sabida  Aisha Indo tana da shiga  rai  sosai.

Tunda ya isa benue suke fama kwantar da tarzoma sai da suka shafe sati biyu suna abu guda sannan suka dawo cikin barrack.


Anan suka had'u da Salmah lkc tana Rose Mary dake itama Babanta soja ne shi Ke gaba da ogansu Khaleed toh akan rikicin Khaleed yaje gidan yanda za'a  shawo kan al'amarin nan ta ganshi tun daga ranar tafada sonshi Khaleed wankan tarwad'a ne dogo ne mai fafad'ar kirji yana da kyau dai dai sannan gashi da kwarjini da cika Idanun mutum yana da hakuri sosai duk cikin gidansu babu mai sanyin halinsa shi yasa Salmah take juya shi san ranta amma yana da fad'a idan aka kure shi da shi da abokansa halinsu d'aya Muktar basu da fad'a har gwara Khaleed ma .

               Tunda rose Mary ta ganshi ta haukace nan tashiga bin duk inda yake sai ta je kuma suna had'awa har su gaisa.

"

         fita tayi daga gidan gidan wata Kawarta taje tana kuka ta fad'a mata halin da take cikin cikin ko inkula tace " bana fad'a miki dole kinime shi ya asiri ko tsafi amma kika k'i Ke gaki mai tsoran Allah sai kiyi kuma "

         Godiya tayi masa sannan tafita da sauri   tafiya me nisa tayi kafin ta iso barrack din tana zuwa kofar gidansu taji anjawo ta kaman zata fad'i d'aga kai tayi ta kalle shi dake bata da tsayi jikinsa na rawa yace " ina kika je banganki ba Ina sonki Plss Zaki aureni "

Bayan sun gama suka fito falo suna cikin abinci Ummi na d'akinta tana shiryawa domin itama falon zata fito. 
Jin jiniyar motar a cikin gidan sujada Ummi tayi tana godiya ga Allah domin ko ba'a fada mata ba tasan khaleed ne ya zo. 
Ayusha ta dailies loma kenan zata kai bakinta yayi Salama sannan ya sako kai ciki "Wa'alakumun Salam "
Suka amsa masa, 
Zuba mata idanun yayi yana karasowa cikin falon. 
Mikewa tayi tana masa barka da zuwa " Sannun da zuwa Uncle " tace masa, 
            Murmusawa yayi kafin yace " yawa sannun ko Ina Ummi take "

       Nuna masa dakin ummi tayi da hannun sabida kwarjinin da yayi ta kasa magana. 



Yana shiga yasami Ummi akan abin sallahr tana hawaye  nazuba daga idanunta da sasarfa ya isa wajenta  yazube yana kuka yana cewa "  Ummi kiyafe min na tuba wallahi bansan yanda haka Ke faruwa  dani ba Ummi Don Allah kiyi hakuri na tuba "



tsakanin d'an da Uwa sai Allah  jawo shi tayi tasaka  kansa akan cinyarta tana Shafa kansa tace "Babana ay ni bana fushi da kai abinda Ke damuna shine matarka wanda bakasan da ita ba shine Ke damuna amma ni banyi fushi da kai ba sabida na yarda da kaddara mai kyau ko mara kyau dan haka bana fushi da kai "


        share masa hawayensa tana zolayarsa tace " toh idan Aishatu ta shigo tasameka kana kuka dariya zata maka dan haka kayi shiru yawuce Allah ya kiyayye na gaba "

Mikewa tayi tafito ta same hanaiza ita kad'ai  a falo tambayarta  tayi " Ina Aishatu take "

hanaiza  tace " tana  dakinmu "

      shiga  d'akin  tayi  tasameta  a kwance  idanta a rufe zama  tayi akan  gadon kafin tace " Yanzun  ya kamata mu fara aikin mu  wanda dole  sai  da had'in kanki Aishatu zanfi kowa  farin ciki  idan Khaleed  ya koma  kaman Da ...... fasali taja  kafin tacigaba da magana muryarta na rawa " Aishatu wannan  itace Daman mu  da zamu jawo  Khaleed jikinmu dan haka  kitashi muje ki taimaka min kukai masa abinci"

Mikewa tayi suka  fita  daga  d'akin kitchen  suka  shiga ta  had'a  abinci a babban  tire suka fita  Ummi  na gaba  Ayusha  na baya har cikin dakin ummi suka shiga da salama gabansa  ta ajiye  tiran  sannan  ta had'a masa  kafin tafita  daga  d'akin ta Koma  nasu. 


Kallon Ummi yayi kafin yace "Ummi  Nikam Yarinyar nan  da Y'ar Muktar take min kama  wallahi "


         murmusawa Ummi tayi tana kallon sa tace " ita ce hmmm  Muktar kenan Allah yabi masa bukatunsa  na alkhairi "

"Amin......Hmm Ummi  Nafeesa ce aka  d'aura auran da ita " inji Khaleed ya Ke tambayar Ummi, 


      Girgiza kai tayi tace " Wacce  ta kawo maka  abinci  Itace aka  d'aura auran  da ita  y'ar Amininka Muktar "


zaro ido yayi  kafin yace " Ummi kuma kika barshi Ay ni gaskiya a Y'a na d'auketa ba mata  ba ena Haba dai  Muktar  yana  hauka ne ina laifin Maryam ko Fatima sai y'ar karamar su a'a bana bukatar aurenta  gaskiya...... "

019....page 


Duk wani annurin dake fuskan Ummi sai da ya dakushe wani wawan kallon ta maka masa kafin ta kad'a kai tace " Yayi kyau Khaleed bazan maka  dole  ka zauna da ita ba Amma kasani matsuwar kace zaka rabu da ita hmm tabbas zan hakura daga al'amarin ka domin Allah shi yafi kowa sanin me had'a tsakanin wannan Auren naku har kana  da bakin cewa zaka rabu da ita tayi karama ko akan idanuna kai har k'asan dadin babbar mace ko Khaleed "

wani irin kunya ne ya rufe shi har yakasa motsi🙈

        babu musu ta Mike tasaka hijab d'inta har zata fita Ummi " tace kicire hijab d'in idan Zaki had'a masa ruwan"


gyad'a kai tayi sannan tafita kirjinta yana wani irin bugawa har ta isa side d'insu yana falo a zaune tashigo da sallam d'akinsa ta wucce kai tsaye daman tasan d'akin toilet din tashiga ta had'a masa ruwan,


zata fito kenan sukayi kicebess da ita karewa mata kallon yayi kafin yace " Shekarunki nawa? kin gama scndry skul? kin sauke alqur'ani ne? "

Eh kawai tace masa. 

       Sannan ta rabe gefensa tafita ba karamin kwarjini yayi mata ba ya cika mata Idanunta, 



wajen Ummi ta Koma tana shiga Ummi ta zaro ido tace " Ni Aishatu takwara haka zamuyi dake maza Koma ki cire masa kayan da zai saka. 



Haka ta dawo ta nima masa kayan da zai saka yana fitowa ta nuna masa kayan da taciro masa jin dadin ya rufe shi dama zai samu irin wannan gatan lallai Allah mai kyauta da kari. 


haka Ummi tayi tasaka Ayusha abubuwa domin Jan hankalin Khaleed kuma.


Da dare sai ta tagama shiryawa zata kwanta Ummi tace " Kishiga kitchen ki had'a masa black tea kikai masa kinji ko "

     Haka kuwa ya faru tana zuwa tasamu a rufe tabuga duk suka shareta komawa tayi ta fad'a masa tace " Uncle Ummi ta rufe min gidan bansan ya zanyi ba Plsss muje ka da sasu subude min "

      tana fad'an haka ya ganon manufar Ummi Murmushi yayi kafin yace " ba damuwa toh kikwana anan mana bawani abu "

kaman zatayi musu sai kuma taji kunyarsu kujeran falon ta kwanta akai tun tana gane abinda ake a TV har bacci yayi gaba da ita sai Sha biyu saura yakashe kallon sannan ya sunkuce ta yayi d'akinsa  da ita yana kwantar da ita yashiga toilet ya watsa ruwa sannan yafito yasaka kayan bacci yana Juyawa yayi tozali da cinyoyinta tayi birgima rigar baccin yayi sama.

020....page 


Tun Shigowar Ayusha bata sake shigowa ba kai har yayi wanka yafito ya d'auki kayansa babu alamunta. 



Can da yagaji da zama   jira ko zata kawo masa abin karyawa amma shiru kake ji dole ya fita zuwa cikin gidan yana fitowa yaransa suka shiga kai gaisu hannun kawai yake iya d'agawa  sabida abukace yake son ya gan kwalliyar safe da tasaba masa kafin karyawa.


          Yana shiga yasami Ummi zaune a kafet tana karyawa da salama yashiga durkusawa yayi kasa ya gaishe ta. 


         Sai da tayi lallausar Murmushi kafin tace " Babana katashi lafiya ya zaman sokoto "
        
      Shafa Kai yayi yana dariya sannan yace " Ay Ummi kowa yabar gida toh gida yabarsa Alhamdulillah ",


"A lallai kam Babana naga kama wannan karon zaka mana hutu sosai kenan tunda gashi har yanzu kana sakoto wai yaushe Zaki Koma ne " Ummi tace masa, 



shiru yayi yana nazari abinda Ummi Ke nufi da haka can yace " Ummi wai ina   yarinyar take ne "


Ummi ta fahimci Ayusha yake tambaya birss tayi da shi bata sake cewa kome ba abincinta kawai take ci a ranta kuwa cewa take bazan baka ita a banza ba sai k'asan darajarta kafin na baka ita. 


      Can suna zaune Hunaiza tafito daga dakin Ummi d'auke da kayan abinci da suka ci, 

       kallon hunaiza yayi kafin yace " Hunaiza ina kawarki take ?"

kallon Ummi tayi sannan ta sakai ta wucce domin suna Idanun da Ummi ta maka mata uwar hararar .


        Atake ya d'ago Ummi tashin hankali y'au ake yinta yace aransa wato Ummi kwanton bauna tamasa.

      wani tausayinsa ne ya rufeta Murmushin tayi masa before tace " babu damuwa Addu'a kullun ina kam muku Allah yabaka sa'a kuma kafin jinin ina da magana da kai amma ba y'au ba "

"Allah ya kaimu" yace sannan yafita,

021....page 



Wani irin zufa ke sauko masa a argitse ko nace  firgice had'i da d'imauta ya d'ago kai gabansa yana dukan tara tara gwiwuwinsa ya Zuba a kasa yace " Ummi kirufa min asiri kaman yanda Allah ya rufa miki, ki taimakeni kaman yanda Allah ya taimakeki, Ummi kitausaya min mana yanzun nasan nayi aure indai akan maganar da nayi ne na tuba nabi Allah na biki ,"


yakarshe maganar kaman zayi kuka duk jarumta irin na Khaleed y'au sai gashi akan Ayusha yana shirin kuka. 

Kallonsa tayi sosai kafin tace " kaje ka dawo sai muyi abinda ya dace akan maganar Ay kai ka bata Rawanka da tsalle ni kuma ina tsoran karna baka y'ar mutane ku kasheta gwara tun wuri musan abinyi tunka wankin hulla yakai mu ga dare kaje Allah yabaka sa'a akan duk abinda zakayi,"


    Mikewa yayi jikin a sabule itama d'akinta tashiga ta kalle Ayusha da tafito daga band'akin tace " Maza kiji kuyi sallama da shi kinji"


         hijab Ayusha ta d'auka zata fita Ummi tace " kaman yanda na fad'a miki kiyi masa abinda zai mance da 6acin randa aka masa babu kunya a tsakaninku nima na daukeki kaman Y'ar da nahaifa ne maza jeki "
      gyad'a Kai tayi sannan tafita yana tsaye yana kallon sider din Ummi sai ga Ayusha tafito da sauri ya karasa wajenta partdinsu yajata dak'yar ya bud'e kofar jikinsa har rawa yake yana bud'e kofar ya rufe da jikin kofar ya had'ata sannan yajawota jikinsa yana sauke ajiyar zuciya aka aka yake saukewa can ya d'ago kanta yana kallon cikin idanunta yace " Little mummy kin amince da abinda Ummi zatayi mana nasan Ke yarinya ce har yanzun fa sauranki amma Plss karki bari Ummi ta rabamu kinji ko "
gyad'a masa kai tayi. 



A hankali yashiga neman bakinta Jikinta ne ya d'auki rawa tana girgiza masa kai. 


Zuba mata ido yayi can yayi kasa da murya yace " ba zan miki kome ba sai da yardanki amma don Allah ki barni nayi Koda kiss dinki mana just kiss kinji."

Kifa kanta tayi da jikinsa duk da bata da wani way'o amma tasan me ake nufi da kalmar miji koma tana jin wa'azi akan mace tabi mijinta domin shine hanyar shiga Aljannarta idan tayi akasin haka zata shiga wuta runtsa idanunta tayi kafin ta d'ago kai tana Kallonsa, 



wani farin ciki ne ya rufe shi hannunsa yasa ya zare mata medical glass dinta yayi yasaka a aljuhunsa sunkuyar da Kai yayi dai dai fuskanta ita kam rufe idanunata tayi har a lkc Jikinta bai bar rawa ba had'a bakinsa yayi da nata wani irin Abu taji yayi mata yarrrrrrrrrr hannunta tasa ta dafe kirjinsa yanda tayi bakarami  taimaka masa tayi ba sosai al'amarin Khaleed yayiwa Ayusha girma domin ba sumbatar kad'ai ya tsaya yi ba a'a har da ta6aiya.


_I mean kun gane Ay_🙈


Tun suna daga tsaye har gwiwarsu ta fara gaza daukarsu sabida yanda yake bida y'ar kwaila abin yana tasiri akanta tuntana jin ba dadi har ta fara Na'am da al'amarin Uncle d'inta tana jin yanda hannunsa har yakai cikin rigarta da sauri ta janye bakinta tana me sunkuyar da Kai kasa, 


Murmushi yasake yana kallonta yace " Ayusha ko mu tafi ne naga kaman keman kin son mu zauna tare "

   hannunta tasa ta rufe fuskanta da shi sabida kunyarsa. 

     Riko hannunta yayi suka fito har waje ya kalleta yace " kina son in dawo ko na zauna a can "
yana fad'a haka yana Kare mata kallon, 

Kanta a sunkuye tace " Why not "

tana fad'a haka tajuya da gudu zuwa cikin gidan tana dariya, 


girgiza kai yayi yana Murmushin sannan ya bawa Sojan dake janshi murnar sutafi. 


Tana shiga falo Ummi bata falo d'akinsu ta shige ta fada gado tayi rub da ciki tana Dariya tana tuna irin abinda Uncle d'inta yayi mata wani murmushi tayi tana rufe idanunata, 


a can d'akin Ummi kiran Shuwa tayi suka gaisa tace " Ina son kinema min mai gyaran amare ina son a gyara min takwarata ce"


shiru tayi can tace " toh kizo d'auketa kawai domin nasan dole zai dawo kuma zai bukaci na bashi matarsa ni kuma ina son yasan darajarta  da kimarta kafin tashiga hannunsa ina son naga wutar Sonta a cikin idanunsa ne bana son nabashi ita haka domin ina Xargin ko Sha'awarta yake kin ga kuwa idan na bashi ita yana Sha'awarta yana biyan bukatarsa zai wurgar ta ita sabida ta gama masa amfani shine nake tura mishi ita "

sake Shiru tayi kafin tace "toh D'azun dai da nace ya dawo yabani takarda Sakinta naga tashin hankali a idanunsa toh amma ba damuwa Allah ya mu dace duniya da lahira, "

da haka sukayi sallama  da  Shuwa fitowa tayi daga d'akinta tashiga d'akinsu ta samesu suna hirar azumin da za'a Fara jibi tace " takwara kishirya Zaki gidan Shuwa ki zauna har sai ya dawo kinji "


"toh"tace mata, 

sannan tashiga had'a kayanta  a wani akwauti tsaf Hunaiza tafito mata dashi sallam sukayi drive ya d'auketa sai gidan Shuwa dake g.r.a,

tana zuwa suka gaisa da Shuwa suna cikin hirar Mai gyaran amare tazo d'aki guda Shuwa ta ware musu suka fara aiki baji ba gani. 


******

A can Abuja kuwa tunda suka isa domin Jirgin airline yabi daga sokoto zuwa Abuja tun da suka isa suke meeting har akan fara azumin duk wani abinda Salmah zata bukata ya tura mata don ko waya bayayi da kowa sabida meeting d'in dasuke yana bukatar sirrine shi yasa aka amshe wayarsu.


sai da suka kwashe kwana goma suna meeting kafin suka gama daga nan kaduna ya fara zuwa inda yaje yasami gidan kaman tsohon kango sabida dattin bala'in da shi da wasu yaransa suka kintsa gidan itama matar gidan ta tafi yawon ta zubar d'inta takaice da bakin ciki suka masa diran mikiya girgiza kai yayi kawai sai da suka gama aikin kafin ta dawo ko da tazo ta same shi ko kallo bai isheta ba shima bai bi takanta ba.

*023....page*


Ganin ya yak'yaleta yasa ta tashi da sauri tasa kayanta jikinta yana rawa sake satar kallonsa tayi taga har yanzun yana rub da cikin. 


Hankali ta tako inda yake ta tsuguna tana kallon fuskansa da yayi kaman mai bacci shiru tayi tana kare masa kallon kama daga sama taji yana cewa ....." ya dai matar Uncle ko musake ne dan naga kaman kina so ko...... "
kashe mata ido yayi yana murmushinsa mai k'ayatarwa.


Mik'ewa tayi ta fita da gudu tana dariya kai uncle shi ko kunya baiji ba. 


Tana shiga falon ta sami Ummi a zaune sai lkc ta tuna ashe hijab d'inta a hannunta yake d'ankwali ne kawai akanta rufin asiri dare ne babu kowa a harabar gidan, 


Kaman yajuya sabida kunyar Ummi domin zip d'in rigarta a bud'e yake ta baya hijab d'inta tasaka sannan ta gaida Ummi tashige d'akinsu murmusawa Ummi tayi alamun jin dadin yanda Ayusha tasake da shi kuma ko yanzun ya nemi tafiya da ita zata bashi sai dai ko bai nemi haka ba. 

*******
Da asuban lkc sahoor layin Ummi yakira yace..... " Ummi don Allah Aishatu ta kawo min abincina bazan iya zuwa bane..... "

......"Toh....."kawai tace masa, 

Sannan takira Hunaiza tace........ " kicewa takwara takaiwa Babana abinci Sahoor yana jira...... "

".....toh... "tace, sannan tafita zuwa d'akinsu ta fad'awa Ayusha sa'kon Ummi ba musu ta shiga kitchen ta had'a masa sannan tasa hajib d'inta takai masa yana zaune tashigo har gabansa takai teran ta ajiye kanta a sunkuye tace "......Uncle barka da asuba gashi nan..." tashiga bud'e masa abincin, 


Lumshe idanunsa yayi sabida yanda take magana mi'kewa yayi ya d'agota ya zare hijab d'in wani shaid'aniya rigar baccin tasaka wanda yayi nasaran tafiya da dukkanin nutsuwarsa.


  Ai baisan lkc da ya gigice ba sabida yanda gabar rigar take mai net ne mantawa yayi da sahoor d'aukarta yayi yakaita bedroom d'insa ya rage wutar d'akin kafin ya ra6e gefenta yashiga aikin lada nutse yake kome yanda zata d'auki karatun da sauki sosai ya d'aurata a network d'od'ar sai gashi itama tana maida masa da martani sosai jikin Ayusha Ke d'aukar sa'kon Khaleed yanda ya dace shima yaji dad'in yanda suke sarrafa juna bashi ya barta ba sai da yaji ana kiran sallar asuba kafin ya rabu da ita, amma hajiya babbar sa tak'i komawa dai dai tana up. 🙄


Rub da ciki yayi ma yau, jikinta yayi sanyi tace " uncle meke damunka ne? ko nice bana maka yanda ya dace?...... "


girgiza mata kai yayi dak'yar yace...... " Babyna babu kome kawai dai abina ce bata koma dai dai ba shine yake damuna da d'an ciwo.... "🙈
Juyawa yayi ya nuna mata abinnasa dake cikin 'yar boxer's d'insa.
Shiru tayi kaman ruwa ya cinyeta kafin tace ..."
Toh uncle me maganinsa wannan ai ba abinda zaka zauna da shi bane kaje ka ga likita ko na fad'awa Ummi ne......"


Yaranta mai dad'i😂🤣🤣


Kuyi ha'kuri wallahi bana jin dad'in jikina ne Ohoo y'au zanga masu dariya😀😂🤣
