*024....page*

Zuba mata ido yayi kafin yace.... " kul kar naji kin fad'awa kowa kinji ko in ba haka ba zan fad'awa Abbanki..... "

Ganin yanda yayi maganar babu wasa a ciki yasa ta sunkuyar da kanta kasa tace...... " kayi hakuri bazan sake ba .... " 
  yanda tayi maganar dole yabaka tausayin mikewa yayi yaja hannunta suka fito falo zama yayi yaci abincinsa sannan yasha ruwa tea sai da yagama sahoor sannan. 

Yace..... "anjima zan koma kaduna bazan sake dawowa ba sai anyi Sallah da kwana biyu zanzo kinji ko My Babe....."

          A birkice ta kalle shi kama zatayi kuka tace.... " uncle toh yanzu waye zai namin abinda kake min ni wallahi sai dai mutafi tare haka kawai zaka tafi kabarni bayan k'asan abinda kake min yana min dadi wallahi zan fad'awa Ummi katafi dani....."🙈🙊
Ay kuwa tasaka masa kuka sosai ba karamin mamaki yasha ba dama tana  iya magana haka.



Tasowa yayi har gabanta yajawota ya runguminta yace..... " karki damu zan dawo natafi dake Ay saura y'an kwanakin ne sallan kiyi shiru karki fad'awa Ummi ga tsarabar da nazo miki da shi.... "

      Waya ce techno mai kyau Babba yasaya mata sai iPad ya bata murna take har da tsalle kaman zatayi hauka take abinda Shuwa ta fad'a mata ya dawo mata cikin brain dinta ajiye wayar tayi ta rungume shi tace ......" Thank you uncle Iluv....."
boye fuskanta tayi a kirjinsa tana dariya kasa kasa wai taji kunyarsu 🤔


dad'a runguminta yayi sosai dai dai kunneta yace.... " Iluv too my baby doll ....."

Haka suka bata lkc suna tsaye su basu zauna ba kuma basu rabu ba duk Khaleed yashiga damuwa ba karya yanda suke haka ba karamin haifar masa da damuwa yake ba domin kullum da son kasancewa da ita yake kwana kuma da shi yake tashi kiran sallahr shiga yasa ya saketa yashiga toilet yayi wanka sannan yafito yace.... " ki shirya min kayana zan dawo..... "

wardrobe dinsa ta bud'e ta had'a masa kayansa sannan.


Ko kafin ya dawo har ta gama had'a masa kome sannan tasake had'a masa ruwan wanka yana shigowa tace..... " uncle barka da asuba kashiga na had'a maka ruwan wankanka a ciki toilet..."

Ba musu ya shiga yayi kafin yafito tacire masa uniform dinsa na aikin tunda taciro tasa kuka har ya fito rarrashinta yake har ya gaji yafita da kayansa yaransa suka zo suka amsa sannan ya koma ciki y'ar rigimarsa. 

Koda shiga bata bar kuka ba har da birgima duk rigarta ya yaye sama zuwa yayi ya d'agata amma bata fasa ba itafa sai ya tafi da ita fita yayi wajen Ummi har d'akinta.

Yana shiga ya tsuguna kasa ya gaishe ta.

Amsawa tayi cike da jin dadi sannan ta tattaro hankalinta gare shi tace...... " Babana lafiya ko har kashirya ne ena takwara take...... "

Sosa kansa yayi kafin yace........ " Ummi wallahi tunda na fad'a mata zan tafi take kuka har da birgima tana cewa zata bini....."

Yakarshe maganar cike da jin kunya.



    Murmushin jin dadin Ummi tayi sannan tace..... " tashi katafi Allah ya tsare ya kare ka yaba da sa'a karka koma d'akin barta tayi rikicinta zatayi Shiru....."


      Haka ya mike yafita jikinsa a sanyaye yayi mata sallama yafita daga wajen yana jiyo sautin kukanta motarsa aka bud'e masa yashiga sai kaduna. 


Tun tana saran zai shigo har tayi bacci a wajen. 

*******

A wani gidan man total suka tsaya su sha mai toh wajen akwai masu bara dan haka ya fida kud'i ya raba musu sadaka binsu yake yana bawa kowa y'ar yazo kan wani tsoho ya bashi zai kara gaba tsoho yace.... " Allah yakarbe ya kareka daga Sharrin mutum da aljan sannan kayi hakuri da kaddaranka kayi kokarin hakuri da duk abinda zaka gani ko wanda za'a  fada maka mungode.... " murmusawa yayi kafin yace... " Nagode Baba sannan  ya koma wajen motarsa dake har sun sha mai. 

Tunda yashiga mota ya manta da wani magana. 


Sai biyar suka isa lafiya kafin yashiga gida ya kira Ummi ya fada mata sun isa lafiya 

Zaune suke ita da Kawarta Aina'u ta kalleta tace " Salmah wai ena mijinki yake zuwa ne kullum yana hanya nifa ina zargin ba ganinsu yake zuwa ba ko Uwarsa ta masa wani Auren ne...... "

Ay tana rufe baki suna jin dirin Motarsa tashi tayi tace.... ' Nayi magana kinyi banza dani amma idan ta baci zaki nime ni.... "

"A'a Aina wallahi naji tsoran maganarki ce aure fa hmmm rufa min asiri Ay wallahi duk Wacce tashigo tsakanina da Mijina zan kasheta ko na haukatata .... "
Salmah tace, 

Khaleed yana shigowa suka gaisa da Aina'u  sannan Salmah ta rakata Aina tace..... Kawata wallahi ki tambaye shi in ba haka ba humm Zaki Sha mamaki ne Allah  namiji bashi da tabbas kuma kinsan Uwarsa tana tsaye akansa sai anjima..... "

tana  tafiya a zazzafe  Salmah ta Koma cikin gidan d'akinsa ta nufa ko sallama babu tace " Khaleed ena kake zuwa ne kaje kayi kwanaki kuma ba aiki ba ehhe.... "

Kare mata kallon yayi kafin yace " Sokoto nake zuwa kuma na manta ne ban fada miki ba...."

A razana tace..... " me zaka fad'a min nashiga uku "

"Aure nayi a can bayan sallah zan kawota nan kafin jarabawarta tafito.. "

Hannu bibiyu tasaka akai ta zunduma ihu tana kuka ni zaka yaudara eyye ni zaka cuta shine zaka min kishiya wallahi kaci amanta kuma sai na hanaku  jin dadin juna itama munafukar Uwartaka sai nasa ta kuka......."

Mikewa yayi ya d'agota sannan ya kifeta da mari  sannan ya turo ta waje. 

Tunda daga ranar  kwanciyar hankali  da zaman lafiya suka bar gidan Khaleed inda Salmah  tasaka shi a gaba da bala'i da masifa na y'au daban na gobe daban. 


A haka  har akayi  sallah dama  har addu'a  yake  Ayi sallahr  ya gudu sabida  fitina sai  ga aikin  ya rike shi 

******
A can kuwa  tunda ta farka  ta ga  ya tafi  wani kukan tasaka sai  da Ummi  tazo da kanta  har d'akin   tasameta rarrashinta tayi sannan  suka fito sosai Ummi  tasake  Sata  a gaba  da  gyara sosai  itama  har akayi  sallahr  babu  shi babu labarinsa. 

Can  shima  dak'yar  yasami  hutun  sati biyu ko gida  bai Koma  ba suka daukin  hanyar  sokoto. 


7:15pm yashigo  gida gidan  da mugun gudun ta rungume shi tana Ajiyar zuciya sake  runguminta yayi shima  gyara murya  Ummi  tayi  take  wani  kunya  yarufe su dukkansu boya tayi  a bayansa tana  dariya  itama  Ummi  dariya tayi kafin tace " toh shiga  kitchen  kidauki abincinsa  kutafi nidai  nagaji ko tafiya  kaduna zakayi  katafi  da ita "
gudu  shige kitchen  ta d'auki abinci suka  fita domin  zai gaida  Ummi  tace " jika  gobe ma gaisa fita sukayi tace " Allah ya albarkacin rayuwarku" tana Murmushi.

Wanka yashiga yayi sannan  yafito  ya gabatar  da sallah dake  akansa  kafin  yaci  abincinsa yana gama  ci ana kiran sallahr  Isha'i fita  yayi  masalaci  bayan  an idar yafita  cikin gari  yasayo kayan abinda ake  shiga  D'akin  amarya  da dashi  ya dawo  yasameta  a falo  tana  kallon  wani  film  sa sauri  tazo  ta amshi  abinda  Ke Hannunsa sannan  ta  ajiye shima  zama  yayi  suka cigaba  da kallon  film  can  yace " kijuye  mana  abin dake  ke cikin  laidan nan muci " babu  musu  tayi  yanda  yace.

Haka suka ci tana Zuba masa santi  shi kuma  yana  dariya suna gama ci takai  kitchen  sannan ta dawo suka cigaba  da hirar  har past 11 sannan  yajawota  suka shiga  daki wanka yashiga  yayi sannan  itama  tayi shafe shafe  take tayi domin haka takwaso  kayanta  ta dawo dasu  part dinsu Tana  gamawa  ta kwanta  a kusa  da shi. 

Karatun da yake mata ya d'aura mata sosai sukayi kewar juna daga nan  karatu ya sake salon hmm Ay fitowa nayi  a 360 sabida bazan iya ba daukowa ba. 

Tun tana  iya d'aukar abinda yake mata  har tafara kuka tana wallahi bazata iyaba hakuri take bashi amma ina yayi nisan kiwo domin ba fahimtar ta yake  ba Ummi kuwa tasha kira Mummy da  Abbanta kuwa sun sha kira daga nan fa ta lura bai saurareta ba tace " wayo  uncle  na tuba kayi hakuri  wallahi zan mutu zanyi amai "

Ay  Khaleed bai saurara mata ba sai da yasami nutsuwa sosai dan lkc ko numfashin bata iya.......

EDITING BY UMMERHERNY NERSERLLERH

*025....page*




Gefenta ya koma ya kwanta yana mai da numfashin duk da bawani dogon lkc yayi ba amma taji ajikinta. 





Dak'yar ya mike ya zura jallabiyarsa yashiga toilet ya had'a mata ruwan zafi sosai sannan yazo ya d'auketa yashiga da ita akan wani stool ya sata ta zauna sannan yasa hannunsa a ruwa yaji zafin yayi dai dai kafin ya d'auketa cak yasata kuka ta fashi masa da shi tana yunkurin Mikewa amma ina dannata yayi har sai da ruwan ya ratsata sosai ajiyar zuciya tasake ture shi tayi tana share Kwalla kafin ta cuno masa bakinta gaba tace "  

Ni wallahi zan fad'awa Ummi ko Abbana tunda baka sona kami......."

Murmushin yayi mata kafin yace " toh y'ar albarka duk yanda kika ce ba damuwa Allah yayi miki albarka nagode sosai kin shayar dani Zumarki mai dadi da zaki ga nagarta a tattare da shi Aishatu bani da bakin Godiya sannan bani da abinda zan biya ki da shi amma Ubangiji ya bani abinda zan biyaki da shi Aishatu nagode nagode nagode sosai Allah yabarmu tare dake Allah yaraya min ke yasa ki haifa min kywawan Yara masu nagartarki"


Jikinta ne yayi sanyi sosai sabida addu'ar da yake mata.


Fita yayi yabarta ta karasa sannan ya zo tasauya zanin gadon ya shimfida sabo kafin ya Koma har ta gama zuwa yayi ya sake daukota. 

kitchen yaje ya had'a mata tea mai zafi sannan yazo ya bata da magani tasha sannan shima yashiga ya kintsa kansa ya fito yasamu har ta tuntsire baccin Wahala yayi gaba da ita. 




    Gefenta yabi ya kwanta ya jawota jikinsa sannan suka cigaba da bacci sun. 

      Washe gari da asuban yarigata tashi wanka yasake sannan ya tafi masalaci yana dawowa gida Ummi yaje ya gaida ita sannan ya fito. 


yana shiga falon yaji tana kuka kasa kasa shiga d'akin yayi da sauri yasamu zata sauko daga gadon ne zafi ya dame shine take kuka zuwa yayi ya d'auke cak yayi toilet da ita. 

tare ya had'a musu ruwan wanka turo masa baki tayi irin ita bata Yafi k'i kulata yayi sata yayi a cikin bahun wanka sannan shima ya shiga hmmm ba wanka akayi ba Soyewa Khaleed yayi da yarinya karama Sosai tun tana buge hannunsa har ta hakura sabida sakon yana isa inda ya dace. 




Bayan Sun gama suka fito dai dai yanzun take takawa amma a hankali. 

Idan yaga yanda take tafiya Murmushin yake sabida shi yasa yanda yake ji. 

Tana shiryawa gidan Ummi ta gudu a falo tasame dak'yar ta durkusa ta gaida Ummi kallo d'aya ta mata tamike ta kama hannunta suka shige d'akinta wani hadi na daban ta had'a mata a ruwan zafi takaita band'akin tace tashiga cikin ruwan kafin tafito tasake had'a mata gaushe da wani turare shima hadinsa daban ne kafin ta zarce kitchen tasa hunaiza ta d'aura mata farfesu da yaji kayan hadi . 


tana fitowa tasata ta tsuguna akan gaushin hayakin yashiga Jikinta sosai sannan ta mike. 



Tea Ummi ta had'a mata da soyayin kwai da bread. 

Tasha sosai kafin ta bata wani tsumi. 

Tana Sha Ummi tace " maza kwanta ki huta Allah yayi miki albarka sannun "

Ay kuwa tana kwanciya bacci yayi gaba da ita. 


*****.
Yana fitowa yaga d'akin wayam dariya yayi yace" Sarkin rigima an gudu kenan shiryawa yayi shima yafito sai wani kyali yake😜

*26.....page*




Shima fitowa yayi yashiga cikin gidan yana shiga ya ga Ummi tana zarya tsakanin kitchen da d'akinta durkusawa yayi kasa yace " Barka da hantsi Ummi "

Cike da jin dadi had'i da farin ciki akan fuskanta tace " Yawwa Babana ya bak'unta "
         Murmushi yayi kafin yace " Ummi ena y'ar rigima take " sai kuma kunya ya rufe shi yayi k'asa da kansa yana wani sirritaciya Murmushi wanda shi kad'ai yasan dalilin yinta. 


Itama Ummi kauda kanta tayi daga Kallonsa tace " hmmm tana bacci a d'akina lafiya  *?*"
ta tambaye shi, 

      Girgiza mata kai yayi mikewa yayi yace " Ummi zan fita zuwa cikin barrack ne akwai wani gida da aka bamu zan duba naga ko da akwai wani gyara da za'ayi a ciki, "

          "Masha Allah a dawo lafiya Allah yakare " tace masa, 

        gaza fita yayi yana kame kame can ta juya zuwa kitchen tana Murmushi jin dadin  yanda lkc guda ya susuce akan yarinyar da ya raina, 

         Tana shiga shima ya fad'a d'akin tana cikin bargo ta kundun dune tana bacci cike da jin dadi murmushi yasake kafin ya isa bakin gadon yaye bargon yayi ya kare mata kallo wani sabon yanayin yasake fad'a ganin yanda rigar ta baiyana kirjinta bai San lkc da ya kai hannunsa kan boons dinta ba a hankali yake murza mata yana wasa da su jin wani yanayi da take tunanin mafarki take yasa ta k'amk'ame Jikinta wani salon ya sake Ay a 360 ta bud'e ido tana ganinsa ta bud'e baki da niyyar kurma masa ihu ya had'i bakinsu yayi mat 🤣


luf ta lafe a jikinsa ohhh gogan tuni ya fara neman sakin layin *Mtn* zuwa *Airtel*😂

ay ture shi tashiga yi tana shirin raba bak'insa dake had'i dankasa ya janye ba don yaso ba duk ya birkice idanunsa sun kad'a sunyi jajur, 
Dole ya barta ko dan ciwon jikinta amma in ba dan haka ba Yasin sai ya ketari iyaka. 🤒

        Murmushi yayi mata yace " princess bari naje na dawo ko " Kallonsa batayi ba sai ma tura masa baki da tayi jan bargon tayi tarufe har kanta ita a dole tana fushi da shi akan abinda ya faru. 
Fita yayi fuskansa d'auke da murmushi kowa ya gansa ya ga sabon ango sabida yanda yake wadace da fara'a.

******
A kaduna kuwa Khaleed yana fita Salmah takira Aina'u tana kuka tafada mata tabbas Khaleed yayi aure  wannan tafiyar da yayi zai zo da ita, 

       shirya fita sukayi zuwa gurin wani kasungurmin boka a birnin gwari   kafin suyi magana yakece da dariya Kaman wani mahaukaci sai da Salmah ta tsorata kafin yace " Uwar mijinki da Abokinsa suka had'a hannun wajen karya wanca asirin har sukayi nasaran aura masa D'iyar abokinsa kinzo aka kasheta ko toh bazata mutu ba amma zata wahala domin akwai rabo a tsakaninsu idan kikace a kasheta da karfin tsiya toh kece zaki mutu kibari ta zo ki maida ta baiwarki kirabata da Mijinki kisa ya manta da alamarinta yanda sai tayi da nasanin aurenshi shine abinda za'ayi domin babu wanda ya isa ya kasheta sai wanda yayita"

yana gaman fad'a haka ya gintse fuska kaman bashi yayi magana ba, 

         Aina'u tace " dan kutumar uba wanda jahannama ne makomarka tsinane la'ananne kai wutace makomarka karaba Uwa da Yayanta karaba Miji da Mata  karaba wasu da rayuwarsu karaba wasu da farin cikinsu ka haukata wasu ka d'ad'ata wasu meke ganin ya dace Ayi duk yanda kayi dai dai ne "

ta kuma hana shi ita sabida lura da tayi yanda Ayusha take tsoransa.😂


     Yau takama asabar Ummi zata islamiyya d'an haka da wuri ta shirya sabida karfe tara take shiga aji kafin tara har tafita tayi sallama da Ayusha. 

  Itama kwanciya tayi abinta. 

    Ashe gogan yana kallon lkc da Ummi ta fita da kwarin gwiwa ya zarce cikin gidan yana tafiya yana wani rangaji irin y'au zan kwashe garan da Ummi take min. 😝

  Yana shiga yasamu bata falo kitchen ya leka Hunaiza ya gani tana aiki gaishe shi tayi sannan yajuya d'akin Ummi yana d'aga labule ya hangota tayi d'ad'a akan gado Murmushin farin ciki yayi sannan ya tura Kai da sallama a zabure ta mike tana zare ido hijabinta ta jawo tasaka ta shiga yarfe  hannunta kaman wacce taga wani abin cutarwa.

      Duk da a tsorace take ganin abinasa babba ne yasa tafara kuka tana neman kwace kanta kafin tayi wani yunkuri tuni ya isa.🙊

       Zafin da taji bai kai nafarko ba don haka bawani dogon kuka ta masa ba a nutse yayi kome ya tashi yajawota yana rarrashinta kukan shagwabe tasa masa ita a dole yayi mata da zafi a tare sukayi wanka duk da kunya yasa tak'i sakin jiki da shi domin a cikin bayin soyewa Khaleed yayi son ransa.

      Toh ta shafa a k'asan hancin ta Insha Allah kome aka boye a cikin gidan zata ji warin toh tabi inda warin yake a nan zata samu kome me ne akayi indai sihiri ne ko tsafi akwai aiki agaban yarinyar ka fa domin abokiyar zaman ta bata zauna haka ba "

A bakin kofar shigowa ta zuba sannan ta koma kofar ɗakinsa ta zuba sannan ta shige ɗakinta.

     Koda suka iso cikin gidan masu tsaron gidan ne suka zo suka buɗe masa kofar motar shi da ita a tare suka fito sai gaisu  suke mika masa yana amsawa har suka iso bakin kofar shiga cikin gidan yasa kafa kenan zai shiga tayi maza ta ruko rigarsa ta kalle shi kafin ta kaɗa idanunta tace " kai Uncle wannan saurin fa ai Aunty tana nan ba guduwa zatayi ba kamanta bakayi addu'ar shiga gida ba " 


Wani kunya ne ya rufe shi murmusawa yayi kafin yace " kai my princess mantawa nayi kina tare dani duk sauran matan kaman maza nake kallonsu " kaman yatayi masa magana haka yayi mata,


Buɗe kofar yayi tare da addu'ar shiga gidan tare suka shiga da ita yana rike da hannunta.


         Har tsakiyar falon gimbiyar gidan tana hakimce sai wani kamshi take sha koda sukayi sallama dak'yar ta masa musu kallon Khalees tayi sama da kasa kafin ta juya ta kalli Ayusha ganin sai da zuciyarta yayi mugun bugawa 💓 sabida kwarjinin da cika ido da Ayusha ta mata,



        Kaman bazata ci kome ba tace " barkanku da zuwa ki zauna mana " tacewa Ayusha, kafin ta juya kallonta zuwa ga Khaleed tace " Honey barka da hanya muje kayi wanka sai ka huta kafin kaci abinci ko."


          Abin mamaki ya bashi sosai domin bai manta artabon da sukayi ba kafin ya tafi yanzun kuma ita ce take binsa da lalluma Alhamdullillah yace aransa.
    
          Murmushi yayi mata kafin yace " muje amma ki kai Ayusha ɗakin kusa da naki sai kizo kisameni ko "


        Kaman yanda ya umarceta haka tayi har da nunawa mata duk abinda take bukata kafin ta fita .
       Hararar kofar Ayusha tayi kafin kwafa tace " da sannun zanyi maganinki hmmm"
          komawa wajeɓ Khaleed tayi ganin ya tsalaka magani tayi wani ihu ta shiga ɗakin tasame shi yana cire kayan jikinsa wani wawan runguma tayi masa tashiga masa wasu abubuwa hankalinsa ne yayi mugun tashi dakyar ya kwace kansa yace "
            haba Salmah kiyi hakuri mana na fito daga wanka kinsa daga tafiya na dawo ina bukatar hutawa "

         shiru tayi tana kallonsa ranta ya ɓaci sosai tace " 
         ay dole kace haka wanda yake da mata irin wanca yarinyar yaushe zai kula irina ba damuwa ai amma kansan hakina ne ko "

     Ga baki ɗaya sun manta da wata sai wajen karfe shida suka fito domin lkc salla yayi Allah sarki ba dan ta rage sauran kazar da yasaya masu a hanya ba da yunwa ya kasarata domin da gayya Salmah ta k'i fitowa ita Ayusha da taga shiru koda suka fito tana ɗakinta tana shirya kayanta a wardrob a nan taciro kayan maganin da Abbanta ya bata tasaka domin a yanzun bata ga amfaninsu ba.🙄



       Da kansa ya shigo ɗakin tanata faman gyara ɗakin bata ji shigowarsa ba kawai sai ji tayi anyi sama da ita ihu tasaka tarike hannunsa dakyau.

         Dariya yake mata inda ya matseta a jikinsa yace " matsoraciya kawai "
      

    sauketa yayi yana dariya kin dariya tayi ta tura masa baki gaba yana ganin haka ya tuna abun yayi rungumeta yayi yana murmushi yace " kiyi hakuri kinji bake ɗaya bace ynz ko kina son na zama marar adalcin ne kiyi hakuri my babe doll "

      aikin rarrashinta yashiga har tasauko tana dariya.

        Dai dai salmah tafito kenan daga ɗakin ganin yanda Ayusha ta maida hankalinta akan littafin jikinta yayi sanyi a ranta tace dani kike yarinya bakisan wacece ni ba,

Salmah ta tambaye ta,

        "Kaman ya kenan kina nufin ko da asiri ko tsafi yana iya karewa sabida addu'o'in kome "


          dariya taba Ayusha tace " Aunty karki damu duk abinda yake ciki ayar alqur'ani ne sai addu'a wannan sun fito ne daga bakin da baya karya Abu alqassem manzon Allah,(s.a.w) babu tsafi ko sihirin da zai tasiri akan mutum matukar kareke azkar da yarda Allah."



           " Toh Allah yasa mudace " salmah tace,


          shiru sukayi hankali ayusha yana kan littafi hannunta kowa ɗaki salmah tayi rai a ɓace takira Aina'u kawai sai ta fashe da kuka tsaf ta kwashi abinɗa ya faru ta faɗawa Aina'u,


       Murmushin mugunta Aina'u tayi kafin tace " karki damu inta san wata bata san wata ba kinji ko "

     rarrashinta Aina'u tayi har ta kashe wayar dariya Ainau tasaka tace "


        Shegiya kifitar da ita nima na kashe aurena na fitar dake wawiya kawai"

Cikin satin Khaleed ya tsiro da wasu hallaya wanda Ayusha bata san yana dasu ba haka kawai zai hauta da faɗa a gaban salmah kaman zai rufe ta da duka a madadin tayi fushi sai dai tayi murmushi .


         Tace kayi hakuri uncle bazan sake ba haka zatayi ta bashi hakuri har sai taga ya sauko take hakura .


          A gefe guda abin yana bawa salmah bakin ciki amma ta zuba ido taga yanda hak'anta zai cima ruwa,

   
      Ana cikin haka wani sabon yanayi yasake faɗuwa Ayusha na ganin jini ko ba lkc watanta ɓa baya tashi zuwa sai ranar da khaleed yake ɗakinta ga wani irin zafin ɗa take ji a jikinta .

    A madaɗin ya tausaya mata sai ya shiga cewa tana masa wari har ya ɗaina shigowa ɗakinta lkc lkc sai dai ko idan jaraɓarsa ta motsa to koda bacci take babu irin ɗan sakon nan haka zai afka mata.😥


      Wani lkc har ciwo yake jimata haka zatayi ta kuka sabida azaba yana gamawa zai cikawa rigarsa iska.



Sam rayuwar gidan babu daɗi abu ɗaya yake sata samun nutsuwa shine alqur'ani toh duk wani abinda take ji da zaran ta fara karatu nemansu take tarasa babu zafin babu zubar jinin dan haka ta maida karatun alqur'ani abin yinta dare da rana.



Duk yanda Salmah taso sanin ko akwai wani abinda ke haɗa su da khaleed Ayusha taki bata fuskan akan haka dole ta hakura .

      Daga baya itama ta tsiro da nata kalan rashin mutuncin inda take saka Ayusha gaba da ayukan ga shi ta tara kawayenta suyita ma Ayusha rashin mutunci.


Abubuwa marar sa daɗi take gani sam khaleed indai ba bukatarsa ba baya tunawa da Ayusha sai abin ya ciyo shi yake tunawa da ita😏




******

Kimanin wata uku kenan da shiganta wannan halin tana kwance wayarta yayi tsiwa ɗauka tayi cike da murna tayi masa sallama ya amsa kuka ne ya kwace mata bai kashe wayar ba sai da yabari tayi mai isarta kafin yashiga rarrashinta yace " Autana faɗa min meke faruwa dake "


     nan ta faɗa masa halin da take ciki shiru yayi kafin ya rufeta da faɗa inda yake shiga ba nan yake fita ba........







Kuma dai🙄

Wai duk akam me🤔

Ga mari ga tsika jaka☹

Bata gigita irin na yau ba lallai tayiwa Abba laifi mai muni tunda yarufeta da faɗa haka neman kukan datake tayi tarasa.

      Wani wawan tsawa ya buga mata wanda sai da wayar ya kunsan faɗuwa yace " ina maganin da nabaki kafin kuzo zan sake rubuto miki yanda zakiyi amfani dasu kinjo ko."

        Kama yana ganinta ta gyaɗa masa kai murya na rawa tace " kayi hakuri Abba bazan sake ba natuba "

          jin yanda take magana yasa jikinsa yayi sanyi babu abinda tabari na wauta irin na Mahaifiyarta Aisha Indo. "Shikenan ki haɗa da na Ayutul Shifa akwai bushashen ganyen magarya amma inso samu ki duba cikin gidan kaman akwai bishiyar a cikin gidan ɗanyen zaifi kyau sosai sai dai in babu kiyi da bushashin yanzun zaituro miki."

        Yana gama faɗa haka yakashe wayar sannan ya shiga rubuta mata text ya turo mata yanda zatayi amfani da maguggunar.



     Ay bata san lkc da tamike ba da sauri ta buɗe wardrob ɗin da ta ajiye maganin ta shiga dubawa wani hawayen farin ciki ya zubo mata kafin ta sake murmushi tace " yanzun zan nunawa wanar matar bata da wayyo ,"

            Kaman yanɗa ya rubuto mata haka tashiga aikin da maganin dake ita take girki kafin ta haɗa abincin take zuba ruwan addu'a sannan ta haɗa masa zoɓo dake yana son zoɓo Salmah bata sha takance ga juice mai zatayi da wani zoɓo.


Cikin kwana uku kome ya fara dai daita da Izinin Ubangiji wannan zafin jikin da zubar jinin duk sun fara ɗaukewa sai aiki na gaba shine yanda zatayi amfani da turaren...

*******
A can sokoto haka kawai Ummi yaki kwanciya sabida tana yawan mafarkin Ayusha tana kuka dan hk yasaka Shuwa a gaba tazo koda kwana ɗaya ne tazo taga halin da Ayusha take ciki toh tace amma sai nan da wani sati sabida akwai wani hidima dake gabanta bikin kanen mijinta duk da dukkansu family ɗaya ne mijin shine babba shine aka ɗaurata akan hidimar.

Toh Ummi tace ba damuwa.

*******
A ɓangaren Muktar lkc da yake wayar da Ayusha yana tare da Hauwa bayan ya gama ne ya faɗa mata abunɗa ke faruwa hankalinta ya tashi amma babu yanda ta iya addu'a suka dukunfa yi.

        Tana son shiga ta ɗuba amma tana jin tsoro.

*33.... Page*





Shiru tayi tana tunanin yanda zata shiga ɗakin dake gidan bakowa mikewa tayi tafita zuwa bakin kofar ɗakin khaleed inda take jiyo warin tura kofar tayi ya jishi gam arufe mamaki ne yatufeta domin a iya saninta Uncle baya rufe ɗakinsa lallai ma girgixa kai tayi domin tasan aikin Salmah ce domin tunda ta lura Ayusha bata shiga ɗakinsa shine ta ɗauki wannan saran.

Kusan kwana goma sha tana yunkurin shiga amma haka bata samu ba dole ta hakura.
          *******
A sokoto kuwa Muktar zaune a gaban tsoho yana masa bayanin yanda sukayi da Ayusha murmushi tsoho yayi kafin ya kishingiɗa yace.
          " Daman nasan haka zata iya faruwa domin jikartawa tafi kowa shiririta "
      Calbin hannunsa yacigaba da ja yana addu'a sosai kafin ya ɗago kai ya jinjina kai sannan yace " bari nasa a sauke alqur'ani amma ba damuwa mun barwa Allah kome domin shi yafi mu sanin abinda ya dace. "

      Mikewa malam yayi yafita ya jima kafin ya dawo yace "   Muktar jeka abinka babu abinda zai sake faruwa Insha Allahu kuma kace mata duk abinda tasamu ya kona shi da yardan Allah babu kome da zai sake faruwa."


Godiya Muktar yayi sanna ya ajiye abin sadaka fir tsoho yaki karɓa dole ys hakura yayi masa sallama ya dawo sokoto.

           *******
A ɓangaren Ummi kuwa shuwa tashirya tsaf domin zuwa kaduna kayan tuli Inna ta haɗa mata dake Ummi ta faɗa mata haka itama Ummi har da kayan duniya  ta haɗa mata.🙃


Washi gari Shuwa ta niki gari sai kaduna.

         *******

       Dake Shuwa takira khaleed ta fada masa tana hanya da ya dawo daga sallar asuba lkc salmah tana bacci ɗakin Ayusha yaje ya faɗa mata tana zaune akan abin sallah tana addu'a .


         A nutse ya kalleta kafin ya kawu da kansa gefe yace" Shuwa tana hanya ki gyara ɗakina domin anan zata sauka ni kuma zan zauna a ɗakin Salmqh kinji "
            gyaɗa masa kai tayi kawai.

   Bai sake magana ba yafita hawaye ne ya zuɓo mata wai a haka kome ya fara dai daita ƙkenan .

         Mikewa tayi ta kwanta can kuma ta tuna ɗa abin karyawa da sauri ta shiga kitchen ta haɗa simple abin karya sannan ta jera akan dinning sannan ta koma ɗaki tayi wanka kallon kanta tayi a madubi yanda tayi mugun rama doguwar riga tasaka sannan tafito tasamu har ya karya ya tafi aiki guzuma tana can a kwance tana baccin mutuwar zuciya babu sallah babu salati.

        Abin karyawa taci sannan ta gama tashiga haɗa na rana karfe ɗaya saura salmah ta tashi ko karyawa ba tayi ba tafita yawon asara.

      Sai kunsa biyu ta gama abincin taci nata sannan tashiga wanka ta kwanta kin zuwa bacci yayi amma batashi daga kwanciyar ba har aka kira la'asar tashi tayi tasake alwala sannan tayi salla.

        huɗu dai dai taji sallama kuma muryan Shuwa da gidu tafita tana ganinta ta rungumeta sannan ta saki kuka zaman sukayi bata hanata ba tayi mamakin yanda tayi irin wannan ramar sai da tayi kuka sosai sannan tayi shiru dan kanta.
            Shuwa tace " ya isa faɗa min meke faruwa kinyi ciwo ne "
       
          a nutse ta faɗa mata kome har da abinda take shirin yi kuma Abbanta yace kome tasamu ta kona.


      Shiru shuwa tayi sannan tace " ba damuwa bani abinci nakaiwa drive domin ba wani abin kirki yaci ba gara da nazo duk da gobe zan koma dole yau na tayaki wannan aiki."
        Kitchen tashiga ta haɗa masa a wani kula har da ruwa sannan tasaka ta fita ɗaya daga cikin sojojin da suke gidan ne yazo ya karɓa yakaiwa drive abinci.

       Komawa tayi ciki tasame Shuwa tana sallah bayan ta idar taci abinci nan suka shafa turaren suka shiga ɗakin ga wari suna ji amma basu sami inda abun yake ba har shida tayi can shuwa ta shishina kasar gadon wani mugun wari taji si da ta tushe hanci sannan suka ɗaga gadon suka ɗauko yar tunkiya sai wani mutun mutumin jikinsa duk allurai fita sukayi dashi waje suka bawa sojojin su tabbatar sun kona kurmus.


     Dawowa sukayi suka cigaba da dube dube ko zasu same wani babu .

        A waje kuwa ay kuwa bukan masa wuta sukayi wani kara ne ke fita a cikin wutar sai da kome ya kone sai suka zubar da tokar A inda suke fitsari.

     Suna zaune shuwa ta kalleta tace " ni fa har yanzu ina jin warin amma ba'a cikin gidan nan ba mikewa sukayi suka tifa ta kofar baya fili ne babba warin suka cigaba da ji hr suka isa kasar bishiyar lemon tsami dubu dubawa suke abinda suka lura kaman binnewa akayi dan haka wuka suka ɗuko suka hak'a wajen layun da Gashin Ayusha da ta binne ne duka ciro shima kaiwa Sojojin sukayi aka kona.

         Murmshi shuwa tayi suka shiga sukayi sallah .

        *******
Yana cikin mota wani zazzabi ne yarufe shi me karfi dake bashi ke jsn motar ba da sauki amai yashiga kwarawa sosai dai da yayi kafin yashiga atisshawa can dai ya kwanta yana tunanin drivensa sai sannu yake jira masa a hka suka isa gida jikinsa babu karfi yashiga.

        Dai dai lkc Salmah itama ta dawo daga yawo😏

     yana shiga yasame Shuwa da Ayusha dai hirarsu suke da sallams yashiga duk suka amsa masa a kujera ɗaya yazauna yana kallon Ayusha kaman wanda yataso daga cutar makanta ko gaisuwar da Shuwa take masa bai amsa ba.

Sai da tasake sannan ya amsa yana sosa kai mikewa yayi yashige ɗakinsa ya akayi ya manta da ita haka duk abinda ya faru yashiga dawo masa brain ɗinda Innalillahi....ya dafe goshinsa.

Suna zaune tashigo babu sallama ko kallansu batayi bayi ba.

         Kallon Ayusha Shuwa tayi sannan tace" Yanzun zaki fara aiki domin dole ki kwace kanki a hannunsu karki bari ta gano lungonki sannan shima Yaya kimasa gashin k'uma ke da shi a ɗaki itama kuma idan ta kawo miki wargi kiji da ita karki mika masa kanki kaitsaye "

         haka tacigaba da ɗaurata akan network har yafito yatafi sallar Isha'i suma sallar sukayi sannan cigaba da hirarsu har ya dawo .

          tunda Salmah ta dawo tashiga ɗaki bata fito ba har suka gama hirar kowa yashiga ya kwanta shima ɗakin  salmah ya shiga ya kwanta dake jikinsa akwai zazzaɓe kota kanta bai biba haka ya kwanta cikin ikon Allah bacci mai karfi yayi gaba da shi.

_Mmn Sadiya Ayusha ta gaidake ita da Salmah_😜

Masoyan Salmah naga kunfi Na Ayusha da Khaleed Yawa fa😎

*34.....page*




Ganin yanda ya kwanta ko bin kanta baiyi ba yasa ranta yasake masifar ɓaci dakyar bacci yayi gaba da ita.


******
Washi gari bayan sallah asuba Ayusha ta shiga kitchen ta haɗawa Shuwa abin karyawa domin seven zasu bar kaduna da gayya ta haɗa abinci daga shuwa da drive sai ita.


Bayan ta gama ta ɗauki na shuwa kai mata ɗaki sannan tafito ta bawa driven nasa itama ɗakinta ta shiga da nata abincin sannan tayi wanka tafito tashirya cikin wani riga da wando pakitans ruwan peachi colour yayi mata kyau sosai sai ya fito da yarintarta sosai barin Abincin tayi ta koma wajen Shuwa wanda ta gama fita kawai zatayi  sami khaleed a ɗakin suna tattaunawa da Shuwa ganinsa yasa zata koma yayi maza yace. "

           Shigo mana zaki koma ina. "
               Ba musu ta shiga ta zauna sannan tace " Ina kwana Uncle "

         " Lafiya lau Aishatu Yar albarka ya hakuri damu" yace mata basarwa tayi bata kara cewa kome ba itama shuwa sharewa tayi kaman bata san kome ba mikewa shuwa tayi tace ..."


Toh bako zayi halinsa Ya khaleed ga Amana nan inji Ummi wai nasake tuna maka Allah yabaku zaman lafiya."


Bin bayanta sukayi Ayusha tana goge hawayen dake zubo mata   jikinsa ne yayi sanyi sabida yasan tabbas ya zalumce ta sosai.

               Jawota yayi haka suka fito har inda motar take tana kuka kasa kasa bankwana sukayi inda Shuwa ta kara bata hakuri sosai.

           Suna tsaye har suka fita bata daina kuka ba ana rufe gate ta juyo cikin gida tana kuka ɗakinta tashiga ta saka key.🙄



Koda ya biyo ta jin kofar yayi gam ya buga har ya gaji ya kyaleta.



       Karan buga kofar ya tada salmah daga bacci ta fito ta koma sabida kaɗuwa ita da boka yace ko kofar ɗakin Ayusha khaleed ba zai sake kalla ba shine har da magiya.



Komawa ɗakinsa yyi wanka .

Sake fitowa salmah tayi sabida yunwar da ta sassikota dakyar tayi brush ta nufu dinning table wayam  taga wajen kofar Ayusha taje tabuga da mugun karfi tace " Ke fito "



     Ba musu tafito tana taunar cingam ta cire kayan ɗazun daga ita sai wani gajeran wando na jinsi da wani riga body hook hannun rigar ma kala biyuƴ ne ɗaya hannun siriri ne sai ɗaya hannun irin na full hannun rigar duk cinyoyinta a wajen tana fitowa ta zubawa Salmah eyes.



Ita kanta Salmah mamaki ne yarufeta sabida tsawon wata huɗu bata taɓa saka irin wannan kayan ba sai yau.

         Girgiza kai tayi tace" me kike nufi Ina abin karyawa mun ban gani ba "


" Abisa ala'adan kasar hausa duk macen da miji yake ɗakinta ita take fitowa ta ciyar da mutane gida ba wacce miji baya ɗakinta ba "
     Ayusha tace mata,

           Ba Salma ba hata khaleed ya ji mamakin  yanda maganar ya fito bakinta domin ya bakin kofar ɗakinsa .


Cike da jin haushi salmah tace " kina nufin bakiyi damu ba kenan "

       ɗaga kafaɗa tayi alamun oho.
 
           Abin ba karamin dariya yaba khaleed ba ɓata rai yayi yace " Aisha ina abin karya mun yake "
         
        tura baki rayi gaba tana bubuga kafa a kasa tace " ni banyi ba haka kawai a dake ni a hanani kuka. "

          Yanda kirjnta ke girgiza shi ya tafi da Imani oga khaleed  sonta haɗi da sha'awarta tare da wani kasala suka rufe shi.

        Kallonta ya tsaya yi sosai.

         Salmah tace " Khaleed bazaka tsawata mata ba dai kayita kallonta."


          "Ba zaiyi ɗin uwarsa ke har kin isa kice yayi min tsawa"
     Ta karshin maganar tana murguɗa mata baki,

          Abinda ya fusata salmah kenan ta ɗaga hannun zata mareta khaleeda ta rike hannun yace " bari nayi maganin rashin kunyarta yanzn"

      Ai jin haka Ayusha ta faɗa ɗaki zata rufe yasa karfinsa ya ture kofar  taji tsoronsa Amma sai tayi wani fuska tana kallonsa takuwa yayi har gabanta ya kare mata kallon kafun ya jinginata da jikin bangon yana shinshina jikinta har ya iso wuyarta yakai bakinsa yana tsotsa har ya iso bakinta kuka ne yaci karfinta ta kauda kanta gefe tana kuka tace" Ina warin yake ina zafin yake ina zubar jinin yake babu dukka yanzun na zama mutum kenan shine ka dawo ay baka saba min da haka ba ka nuna min karfinka shine abinda kanuna min "

   Asanyayi ya fita daga ɗakin murmshi tayi tace " haka naje son"


Tunda suka bar salmah  a waje bakin ciki yasa ta tafi ɗski takira  kawayenta ta faɗa musu.



Khaleed nafita suka zo inda suka shiga zugata sosai duna cikin haka tafito daga ɗakinta kallo ɗaya tayi musu tayi murmushi kitchen freeza ta buɗe ta ɗauko ice block ta zuba ruwa a ciki tana zaune tajiyo su suna cewa wallahi kina bani dariya wannan yar cikin tana shirin haukata ki"

       Tana rufe baki aka watsa musu ruwan sanyi kalauuuu.
A razane suka mike rike k'ugunta tayi  tace " kowa yafita kagin nasa afitar daku da karfin maza "
Kuzii kuga yanda  suke ɗaukar jaka da gyalensu😂

Kowa ɗaki tayi abunta tana dariya .

*******
Da dare khaleed yana dawowa salmah takai karan Ayusha zuwa ɗakinta khaleed yaso zuwa kawai sai ya fasa yace " kira min ita Salmah"

     Koda salmah taje tana kan gado tana shan big bom ko sallama batayi ba tace " kizo ana kiranki"

    ta juya abinta kafn taje sai da ta kashe ten minti kafin ta kafin tafita daga ita sai wani mini siket da short vest tana tafiya tana girgiza jikinta shagala yayi da kallonta.

Zaman tayi a kusa da shi tace" gani nan"

cire big bon ɗin tayi tana kallon shi sannan ta wani lashe kaman yanda yara suke indai suna shan alawa😹

*36....page*


Wa zata gani salmahce keta faman haɗawa khaleed abin karyawa murmushi tayi kafin tace " gud mrnq Aunty Same "

   Sabida neman magana a masifance ta juya tace " a gidan ƳUbanki na kwana yar iskan yarinya karama sai tsabagen karuwance fitan min daga nan "

    Tana gama faɗan haka tajuya tafita ɗakinta ta koma can dai tarasa me zatayi tana zaune taji motsin fitowar khaleed za shi wajen cin abinci wanka tashiga tayi sannan tafito tasaka wasu masifafun kaya ingilishi wear jan janbaki tasaka a bakinta wanda yayi dai dai da bakin yafito da sauri tana yarfe hannun tana wash wash har wajensu taje kaman zatayi kuka tace " Uncle nan ɗina yana  kaikayi bansan ya zanyi ba "

    Takarshe maganar hawaye na zuba a idanunta .


        Jikinsa har rawa yake ya jawota yace " ina ne nuna min in duba miki "

         Dariya ne yacika mata ciki ganin yanda Salmah ta koma Allah sarki.
   
            Kirjinta ta tura masa tana nuna masa cikin rigar yace " kiyi min magana mana yanda zan gane kinji "

      boobs ɗinta ta nuna masa ay a360 yacire rigar ya gefar ya ɓalle brz ɗinta ya gefawa salmah a fuska😂


     Yana ganinsu yasa bakinsa toh dai ku kiyasa yanda yake sosa mata tun yana kan hanya har suka manta da wata Salmah a waje cak ya ɗauketa yayi ɗaki da ita.


  🙆🏼  hannun tasaka akai ra fashe da kuka tashige ɗakin tana kuka kowa yasai rariya yasan.....Inji bahaushe.....

Su Oga khaleed tunda suka hige ɗaki Ayusha take neman kwance kanta amma abu ya faskara kuka tasaka masa cak ya bar duk abinda yake yace " kowani irin laifi nayi ya kamata kiyi hakuri kibani hakkina amma tunda baki so bari na tafi wajen wace zata bani " 

       cike da jin haushi ta murguɗa masa baki tace " Ay wallahi sai na faɗawa Ummi kome "

        Tabbas zata aikata amma bari yayi maganin wannan sabon rashin kunyar da ta koya.

     Uniform ɗinsa ya cire yabita har kuryar gadon yace " bari na baki dan na lura rashin baki da banyi bane yasa kike neman rainani."

      Jawota yayi sosai yashiga sarafata son ranshi tunta ware suka ɗirji juna daman an kwana biyu ba a haɗu ba toh sai da suka kusan cinye juna a tare suka shiga wanka anan ma ba sauki fitowa yayi ya shirya ta rakosa ɗakin salmah yashiga taci kukahar ta godewa Allah yana shigowa kallon ɗaya tayi masa tasake fashewa da kuka tace " nagode da cin fuska plss fita min a ɗaki bana son ganinka amma kafin nan kaje ka kalli madubi sai kafita.


Bai k'iba yaje jikin madubi abin mamaki da kunya ya gani wato janbakin Ayusha ne a kumatunsa wani irin kunyar salmah ne  ya mata shi sumsum yafita ya koma ɗakinsa ya wanke fuska yana nazarin abinda ke faruwa a gidansa.


Dole ya kawo gyara  A cikin gidansa da wannan tunani yafita......

Dariya Ayusha tayi har ta godewa Allah....

Zuba mata ido yayi cike da kewarta da bukutarta tsaki salmah tayi tace" daɗin abu bora ce domin ba kai mace me daraja ba balle tasame mijin a hannunta "

     Khaleed ya kallet sannan ya kalle salmah yace " Aishatu me kawayen salmah suka miki kika watsa musu ruwan sanyi haka.

Mikewa tayi tace " Allah ya isa min ban yafe ba "
 
    Ta juya ta koma ɗaki tana kukan takaici.

 
    Murmushi ayusha tayi tace " Uncle goyeni ka kaini ɗakina.


     Juya mata baya yayi ɗagal ta hau bayan.

Zuwa yayi ya sauke Ayusha sannan yajuya zai fita tarike hannunsa tace " da ka rubu da ita ne kawai kaje ka kwanta kokuma kaje ka rarrashi guzumar ta domin ni bani da abinda zan baka ina hutun sallah


Jikin babu kwari yafita yasameta  Ajikin kofar ɗakinsa tana jiransa ganin babu fuska yasa tashiga hankalinta.


Yana shiga ɗakin itama tashi itama.



"Amma khaleed wnna ba adalci bane yarinya tamin laifi na kawo kara sannan ka ɗauki gaskiya ka bata sabida ita yar Abokinka ce ita ko."
Salmah tace masa,


Shiru yayi bai kulata ba cike da bala'i tacigaba da masa ranshin mutunci,

" Kan ubanca wallahi babu inda zai kaiki baki da kafa ne lallai ma kuwa samun wuri kenan bawa da baccin safe "

Tashi tayi ta shiga kitchen.

Aiki Ayusha take a kitchen tana haɗa abinci gefenta mp4 ne kita aikin karatun alqur'ani tare da sheik Abdurrahman Ahamd sudais a cikin suratul Nisa'i tana ji kuma tana binsa.

          Hankalinta yayi gaba kan aikinta karatune kawai a gabanta babu wani abu.


         Kaman ance ta ɗago kai salmah ce tsaye akanta bata san da zuwarta ba sai ganinta kawai tayi a tsaye abin yabata tsoro amma tunawa da tayi da tana tare da kariyar Allah sai tayi adɗu'ar neman tsari tacigaba da aikinta har tagama zata fita sai taji tana shakkar barin kitchen ɗin  tana cikin wannan tunani ta kurawa kofar kitchen ɗin ido  wani abin mamaki yagani shine barbaɗin magani a bakin kofar murmushi tayi da ta karasa wajen ɗurkusawa tayi tana karewa wajen kallo tashi tayi tashiga duba inda ta ajiye sauran ganyen magarya da yake sakawa khaleed a cikin ruwan shansa.


         Tana samu ta zauna ta fara addu'a a ciki sannan ta zuba a kofar.
      Duk abinda ta dafa rabashi biyu tayi ta boye wani tabar kaɗan a cikin mazuɓen sannan tafita zuwa ɗakinta.


       Nan ma zuba sauran ruwan addu'ar tayi sannan tashiga ɗakinta wanka tayi tana mamakin halin salmah ace mutum baxaka dogara da. Allah sai da wani katon wofi haka tayi ta tunani har ta gama shiryawa tayi sallah ta ɗan kwanta bacci yayi gaba da ita.


 Ba ita tatashi ba sai wajen huɗu da wani abu alwala tayi tazo tayi salla bayan ta gama ta ɗauko turaren nan shafa a kasan hancinta tafito ai kuwa da wari tacikaro neman inda warin kefita take ta kofar ɗakin khaleed ta fara zuwa nan ta ɗauko ruwan addu'ar ta zuba sannan tashiga ɗakin taduba a cikin takalminsa ta ji warin nan ma tazuba haka tafito ta shiga kitchen wajen abincin ta duba tasamu shima tazuba bata zubar ba sai da tagama na dare ta zubar shima ɓoyewa tayi sannan ta ɗauki wanda ta ɓoye takaiwa sojojin a waje.

        Tak'i tabar musu na ranar domin abinci ɗaya suke.


Dan haka boye wanda ta girka tayi sannan tafito tana fita salmah tashiga itama domin taji ksmshi sabon girki tayi neman ko ina bata samu cike da bakin ciki da takaice ta fito kicibes sukayi da Ayusha kallo daya tayi mata ta fashe da dariya har da kyalkyalewa tace " ai baki samu ba ko hmm ni da rayeye nan da baya mutuwa na dogara ke kuma da alamun da wani kato kika dogara ba damuwa kowa tasa tafishe shi "

Shiru salmah tayi can tace " gaskiya haka bazai ɓata yuwa ba sabida tsaron kinsa yanda gidan yake fa ta ina haka zai faru hmm."

            Zuba mata ido Aina'u tayi kafin tace " me a ciki tunda akwai tsaro ki aikesu duk lokaci guda yana kome zai faru babu wanda zai zargiki a ciki ko kuma ki haɗa baki da ɗaya daga cikinsu wanda ƴyake kawo miki gulman a da kome kika gani. "


  Wani shewa suka saka kafin tace " magana ya kare amma hanzari ba gudu ba shine dole ina bukatar ya gani da idonsa ko ? "

          " Yo ai dole ki hillaci lkc dawowarsa yazo ya gani dai da idonsa shine biyan bukata "
      Aina'u tace mata, 

   Haka suka cigaba da tattauna yanda zasuyi da Khaleed inda suka fitar da Ayusha.


     Tashi tayi zata tafi Aina'u tace " ki ajiye duk wani makaminki ki watsar kinemin sulhu da ita yanda zaki shiga jikinta ki nuna mata kin ajiye kishi da ita kin ɗauketa kaman kanwa ko y'an shine babban makaminki da zaki yaketa da shi matsuwar kika ce da zafi zakiyi kome shi kamsa khaleed zai ɗagoki ay kin gane me nake nufi ko"
            Ta ɗaga mata gira sama.
      Dariya sukayi sannan sukayi sallama dariya Aina'u tayi tace " ko ban zauna dake a matsayin kishiya ba sai nasa kanwata ta zauna dake kuma tafita min dake shegiya ni ko ban aure shiba sai nasan yanda nayi namuka ɓata dare ɗaya da shi sannan na barwa kanwata sauran domin haka kawai bazaki makale masa ba bayan kina bin wasu a bayan idanunsa. "


Surutu take tafaman yi ita kaɗai sabida son zuciya.


          ****-*****
Kwanaki na tafiya rayuwan ta junawa Matan khaleed yanda suka haɗi kansu bazaka taɓa ɗaukar akwai wata a kasa ba sabida yanda Salmah take jan Ayusha a jiki ita Ayusha kallonta take kawai sabida bata yarda da yanda ta koma ba amma yau da gobe yasa ta saki jiki da ita.

     
        Ganin haka da khaleed ya gani ba karamin daɗi yaji ba sabida yana kaunar zaman lafiya sosai a cikin gidansa.


A yanda  salmah take babu yanda zaka rantse da Allah kace akwai wani abu a ranta sabida hankalinta kwance take haɗa shirinta yanda ya dace.


      ****-****
    A wajen aiki an turasa jahar river yaje yayi wani aiki dan haka da zai tafi bai ɗauki kowacce ba sabida aikin kwana biyar ne dan haka ya shiryar ya tafi yayinda ya bar ayusha babu lafiya domin baisani ba sai da yatafi wani mugun zazzaɓe ya rufeta.

Wuni guda tayi akwance salmah bata lekota ba yana fita itama tafita yawon gantalinta sai dare tashigo har zata shige ɗaki wani tunani yazo mata ta lekata ganin yanda take yasa ta ruɗe takira shi ta faɗa masa halin da ayusha take ciki.




Wani likita a cikin barrack ɗin yakira ya faɗa masa suzo su duba masa ita dake matarsa nurse ce tare suka zo aune aune da yar gwaji sukayi inda suka same kyakyawan sakamako Ayusha na ɗauke da cikin khaleed.



Koda suka gama shi dakansa yakira khaleed ya faɗa masa a gaban Salmah a bazata maganar tazo mata yake take ta fama yi amma bakin ciki ne fal a ranta.



Sabida farin ciki sujadar godiya khaleed yayi yanawa Ubangiji kirari haɗi da godiya.

Bayansu tafi yasake kiran salmah a dakile ta amsa yace " ina take bata wayar nayi magana da ita "


      Wani murmushin bakin ciki tayi tace " tana bacci sun mata alluran bacci sannan sun saka mata drip sabida tasami karfin jikinta "

 "Shikenan ba damuwa Allah yabata lafiya " yace matA, dakyar tace " Amin"



Tun daga ranar khaleed ya saka salmah a tsakiya ko kira yayi sai yace me taci me kika bata me take so haka ya sake rura wutar tsanar Ayusha.


     *****-****

Kwanaki biyar sun cika dan haka kusan a tare suke ta aiki ga garin an tashi da ruwansa basu suka gama ba sai dare domin shigowar dare zasuyi sabida hanyar lokoja da abuja babu kyau ruwa yacinye ɗaya hannun haka suka gama kowacce ta shige ɗaki tayi wanka domin yakirasu yace sun iso suna headquarter barrack ɗinsu ne .


Gashi ana ta ruwa tunda Ayusha tayi wanka tayi sallan ya ɓingire a kan sallaya.

     Ga gidan babu wuta anta da generato yaki tashi feshin ruwa ya taɓa nepa kuma basu kawo wuta ba sabida  tsawa da walkiyan da ake.

        Tunda ya yanko kwanar gidansa zuciyarsa ke bugawa yarasa meke shirin faruwa da shiba.


        Haka suka isa gidan wanda duhun yasame lamban yabo sabida rashin haske.
    Drive ne ya fito ya buɗe gidan babu masu gadi dan haka bai damu ba sabida shi mutum ne da baya takurawa kansa balle kuma yaransa.

Drive na parking yayi masa sallama ya tafi gidansa shima cikin giɗan yashiga har falo babu kowa ɗakin salmah ya leka yaji motsin ruwa sannan yashiga ɗakinsa ya ajiye kayan a hannunsa ya fita zuwa ɗakin Ayusha da Sallama yatura ɗakin.

    Torchin wayarsa ya haska " Innalillahi wa innahillahi rajoun."

*39.....page*




 "Hasbunallahu waalimal wakil."


Jingina yayi da jikin bangon wani jiri ne ya nemi ɗibarsa Ayusha ne zata ci amanarsa ta kawo masa wani kato gidansa a gadon sunnansa sake kallon inda katon yake yayi yana tsaye duk ila'irin jikinsa rawa yake sabida tsoro da fargaba haɗi da nadama mara amfani.


Mikewa khaleed yayi yafita ay kuwa yana fita katon shima yafita a guje jin gudu ne ya sake fito da khaleed kafin ya isa tuni Allah ya bawa katon nasaran guduwa cike da takaice ya koma ɗakin Ayusha tana ta sharan baccinta.

           Ganin yanda gashin kanta yake hautsine yasake kara masa bakin ciki yaye bargon yayi me zai gani tunɓur take wani kululun bakin ciki ne ya tokare masa makogaro tsinketa da mari yayi mai shiga jikin sannan yafincikota yana hucce hawaye na zuba daga idanunsa a razane ya buɗe ido yana dafe fuskanta ganin babu kaya a jikinta yasa ta faɗa tunani taya haka ta faru da riga da zani ta kwanta amma babu kaya a jikinta a cikin mintuna da basu wuce biyar ba tayi duntun cikin kaukausar murya yace " wani ɗan iska kika shigo min da shi gidana har ɗakina me nayi miki haka da kika zaɓe  cin amanata Aishatu me laifina me na gaza miki inda nikike bukata ki hakura na dawo mana duk abinda kike so shi nake miki me na rage miki da zaki min haka tabba kin gama dani kin jima zuciyata ciwo me wuyar warkewa bazan iya rabuwa dake ba amma dole nayi nesa dake dole na koyawa kaina nesa dake koda mutuwa zanyi da Sonki Aishatu dole nayi nesa dake"


      Yana faɗan haka ya hankaɗata yafita daga ɗakin yaso ya koma waje ya tambayesu ina suka je har wani ya shigo gidan shiru yayi baya son y'aransa susan meke faruwa da iyalinsa.


        Duk wannan bidirin da ake Salmah tana ɗakinta tana jinsu  murna kenan abinda take fata ya faru yanzun itace da gidan babu wata bayan ita.


         ****-****
Shi kuwa kato bayan fitansa waki'a ta faɗa masa sai da yafita daga barrack yana gudu wata J5 ta bige shi a wajen suka gudu sabida dare ne babu haske a wajen sai da wasu suka zo wuccewa ne suka hango shi da sun ɗauka yan* fashi ne amma ganin gasu ga barrack yasa suka taimake shi suka kaishi asibiti bayan sun biya kome anan suka barshi.

        *Kowa yayi da kyau zai ga da kyau*☹

     ****-****

Koda khaleed ya koma ɗaki ko zama ya kasa yi sai safa da marwa yake duk tunaninsa ya kasa gano me dalilin da Ayusha ta masa haka a gaskiya ba zai iya saketa ba sabida irin *HALACCI* da Muktar yayi masa ina kome tayi bazai iya saketa ba  ba amma dole yayi nesa da ita sabida bazai juri ganinta kusa da shiba zai sallameta ta koma sokoto tunda nan da  kwana uku zai tafi dafur.

  D'auko wani buk yayi da pen ya zauna yayi rubutu short not sannan ya mike ya cire kayansa yashiga wanka yana shiga salmah tana shigowa ɗakin dube dube tayi har taci karo da wannan yar not ɗin ɗauka tayi har zata  fita sai dawo da shi   ta ajiye a inda tasamu tana murmushi fita tayi tasake dawowa wajen not ɗin ta koma har zata sake ɗauka ya fito daga wanka nema guri tayi ta zauna kallon ɗaya yayi mata ya kauda kai gefe sabida itama ba yarda da ita yayi ba har gwara Ayushan shi yafara Saninta amma Salmah fa wani sabon bakin ciki ne ta turneke shi yace " fitar min a ɗaki "

   Ya buga mata tsawa wanda sai da ta zamo akan gadon da gudu tafita daga ɗakinsa.


Allah sarki Ayusha  duk tarasa me yake nufi da maganarsa inda ya mareta ta shafa tana kuka tana wajen wank sabon zazzaɓi ya rufeta ana ta ɓingire da bacci.


       ****-*****
Khaleed bacci barawo ne yayi gaba da shi cike da mafarkai shi da Ayusha Salmah ta shiga tsakaninsu amma ayusha tayi nasaran tura salmah cikin rami gefe ga wani yana jan Ayusha zai tafi da ita yana son binsu Ummi ta hana shi sai kuka yake yana b Ummi hakuri amma taki kulashk har ta tafi tabarshia wajen yana kuka dafa shi akayi ya ɗago wa zai ga....
A nan ya farka agogon gadon ya duba yaga huɗu saura tashi yayi sallah nafila yana wajen  yana addu'a har lkc sallah yayi masalaci ya mike ya tafi.

        Koda ya dawo ɗakinta yashiga yasamu tana azkar zuba mata ido yayi kafin yajuya ya kalle inda yaga katon take fuskansa ya sauya.


        Yace" kishirya kayanki ki koma sokoto idan nagama bincike zanzo sannan zan rufa miki asirinki na kawo min namiji gidana sannan karki sake ki haɗani da mahaifiyata tunda ba sakeki nayi ba ki iya da bakinki gashi zanyi tafiya bandani ba ko nadawo ko labarin mutuwata tazo muku na yafe miki amma bana son nasake ganinki a kusa dani a rayuwata baki ɗaya.


Yana faɗa haka ya fita yana kuka itama kuka take itace akawa sharrin kawo kwarto gidan nan cire hijab ɗinta tayi tashiga haɗa kayanta daga ita sai wani rigar bacci shara shara sai da ta haɗa kayanta kaf sannan ya zauna tana kuka me cikin rai  sosai shigowa yayi mikewa tayi ta saka hijab ɗinta ganin yanda kirjinta sukayi wani mugun cika haka kugunta suma sun kara girma baisan lkc da ya isa gareta ba cire hijab ɗin yayi yashiga tura hannunsa cikin rigar bacci ita kuwa tace bata san da haka ba rikice ne ya barke tsakaninsu kyale shi tayi dai da yayi nisa  kafin tace " me zakayi da sauran kato ko ka mantane jiya shima yanda ka haukace haka shima yay...."
Baisan lkc da ya tureta ba sannan ya mike zai fita tace " KHALEED wallahi  sai kayi da nasani "


            Jefa mata wani hararar haɗi ɗa ajiye mata wasu file ya buɗe babbar jakarta ya zuba kuka take sosai haka ta mike ta shiga fita da kayanta tana kuka.

*40....page*





Tana cikin fito da kaya sukayi kiciɓes da Salmah tana dariya gyara tsayuwa tayi tace " Jikar malamai da fatan baki takura kanki wajen neman waye ya miki wannan kullin ba ko hmmm toh ay bakowa bane sai ni nan tunda kina da hanyar karya sihiri d sammu da tsafi kin ɗauka zan barki ki zauna lafiya ne godiya zaki wa Allah da kika rayu tunda baki haukace ba ko kimutu baki ɗaya hmm kin ci nasaran shiga gonata amma tunda ya hanɗaki waje ba damuwa nima na huta ki faɗawa wancar tsohuwar banz...." 

Tas ta ɗauketa da mari tana hucce tace " kinyi kuskure shiga tsakani domin da zaran nabar gidan nan har abada bake ba khaleed tun nice a birni zuciyarsa nice first love ɗinsa nice wacce na zame masa khairi hmm idan na bar gidan nan ke da farin ciki da shi sai dai wasu subaki labari amma khaleed ya haramta miki kaman yanda kika shiga  tsakanina da shi kuma duk wani abinda kike takama da shi na gama dasu hhh kin ɗauka naji zafin rabuwa da shine aa sharrin dakika min ke min zafi amma gaki ga duniya ta isheki riga da wando " tana gama faɗan haka ta fitar da sauran kayanta.


ƊDawowa ciki tayi har ɗakin khaleed tace " nazo na faɗa maka abu guda shine ka zama mai bincike kafin ka yanke hukumci ina ji maka ranar da zakayi kuka zakayi da Nasani na barka lafiya "

        Tana fita tashiga motar neman duk wani kuka tayi ta rasa inda ta kwantar dakai a jikin motar.

Jan drive yayi yana tausayin Ayusha a ransa.


Tunda salmah tasha mari ta fecce ɗakinta tana shafa wajen,



Shima khaleed tunda ta faɗa masa haka jikinsa yayi sanyi yashiga nazari karshe watsar da abin yayi amma time to time yana tuna abin a ransa.

          ****-****
Sai karfe biyar suka iso gidansu tace ya ya kaita har ciki ya isa suna shiga Muktar yana fitowa daga ciki zai fita kafin drive ya tsaya tafita da gudu shima ganinta yasa ya tsaya tana isowa ta faɗa jikinsa ta fashe da kuka mai cikin rai riketa yayi suka shiga gida sun same Hauwa tana falo ita kaɗai Adda Maryam da Didi Fatima suna kitchen domin yazun suke kome na gidan zaunar da ita yayi sannan ya zauna a kasa yana rike da hannunta ya zuba mata ido haka hauwa ma yanda duk suka zuba mata ido sai da tayi sosai kafin tayi shiru tana ajiyar zuciya.





Ruwa Hauwa tabata tasha ay kuwa tana sha ta fara yunkurin amai sosai wanda duk jikinta sai da ya mutu.


  Fita Muktar yayi ya sallame Sojar da ya kawota sannan ya tafi asibiti ya hado mata magunguna da ruwa ya dawo yasa mata.

       Kiran wayar khaleed yayi suka gaisa kafin yace " Naga iyalinka tazo cikin wani irin yanayi kuma taki faɗa min abinda ya faru ko lafiya "


        Cike da jin zafin yar abokin nasa yace " ohho ita tasane ni yanzun haka zan bar gari ne na bata wasika ta baka idan na dawo zanzo idan nayi bincike akan abinda ta aikata...."

    Kashe wayar yayi yana kallon Ayusha da take baccin wahala.
            Hauwa tace " Abbansu me ya faɗa maka naga lkc guda mood ɗinka ya sauya faɗa min don Allah.

      Murmushin dole yayi kafin yace " ohhonta yace !"

       Sannan ya faɗa mata yanda sukayi.

             Dariya tayi sannan tace " eh ai wnnan ba wani abu bane sai dai ko aikin Madam salmah.



Shiru yayi suna zaune har lkc sallah yayi duk suka tashi sukayi sannan suka dawo.


Haka lkc isha'i yayi.


Sai goma drip ya kare yana karewa ta tashe da karfinta wanka Mummy takaita tayi da alwala tazo tayi sallah Muktar da Hauwa suka abinci maryam da fatima suma suna gefe cike da tausayin yar kanwartasu.




Bayan ta gama ci ne muktar yace "me ya faru ko nace me ya haɗa ku ke da Uncle ɗinki sannan yace yabaki aika kibani ko."

Gyaɗa mas Kai tayi tace " bansan meke faruwa ba amma jiya bayan dawowarsa ya tadani daga bacci" sai ta fashe da kuka duk abinda ya faru wanda tasani ta fada musu har yanda sukayi da Salmah da yanda sukayi da shi.

   Jikinsu ne yayi sanyi muktar yace " bani wasikar da yabaki."

     Tace " Abba yana cikin babbar akwatina"

     Kallon maryam yayi ta mike ta tafi ɗaukowa files guda huɗu sai wasikar shi ta ɗauka tafito.

*41.....page*


    Ga abinda ke cikin wasikar kamar haka.

        _Assalamun Alaikum_
```Da fatan wasikata ta iso gareka duk da nasan bazaka ji daɗin abinda zance ba amma ina me bakin cikin da nadaman auren karuwar y'arka wanda babu abinda tasani sai bin maza da kawo su cikin gidana nayi fatan Amintarmu ta ɗoure amma kash haka bazai taɓa yuwa ba da zafi da ciwo da kuna kasami wani namiji a ɗakin matarka ta sunnan amma ba damuwa na barta da Allah kuma ta nemawa cikin jikinta Ubansa domin nasan ba nawa bane Na saketa saki ɗaya......Na saketa saki biyu......Ni Khaleed Sagir Tambuwal Na sake ta Uku...ta nime wani sakaran bani ba````


    Wani kara ta fasa tace " wallahi yace bazai taɓa rabuwa dani ba zai nesance amma baza taɓa rabuwa dani ba."

 Kuka take tana rarafawa taje wajen Muktar taje wajen Mummynsu har y'an uwanta.


     Jikin muktar yayi bala'i sanyi haɗi da tausayin yarsa zuba mata ido yayi yanda take kuka duk falon shine kawai ya dake babu hawaye a face ɗinsa amma dukkansu kuka suke.


    Gyara murya yayi kafin ya fara magana cikin sanyin murya yace "  Duk da ya yanke hukumci cikin sauri da zafi haka bazai shafi Dangartakarmu ba sabida yayi min *HALACCI* a rayuwata bana jin zan datse alakana da shi dan ya furta irin wannan kalaman sai dai naji zafin sheganta cikin da yayi da *sakin* ne kawai amma ba damuwa kuma yace daman karki haɗa shi da Ummi ko toh shikenan bazata san ma kina nan balle tayi wani tunani kar nasake jin kinsake maganarsa a gidan nan kinji ko, "
 

Mikewa yayi ya shige ɗakinsa cike da damuwa tunda yanke hukumci babu amfanin tambayar Ayusha itama bata san dalilin sakin ba kuma a halin da take ciki ba zaiyu a matsa mata ba shi yasa yashare ta kawai a hankali zai ji kome.


    ****-****
 
      A can kaduna kuwa kiran Ummi yayi ya lafta mata karya yace ga Ayusha ta zo amma karatu zatayi zaiyi tunda sakamokonsu ya fito zaiyi tafiya zuwa Dafur zai same kaman shekara biyu ko Uku amma Ayusha ta zauna A gidansu yake so.


Addu'a tamasa sosai.



     *****-*****

_Wato maganar nan ne da masu hazancin magana suke cewa tsautsayi takam bi tuwo har ciki_



Faman haɗa kayansa yake cike da bakin ciki da damuwa domin ji yake kaduna ta masa zafi haushin kowa yake ji mostly masu tsaron gidan yana kaucewa kowa dan haka k'i fita ko ina yana ɗakinsa yana jinyar zuciyarsa.

Sai ga Salma ta faɗo masa ɗakin babu  sallama rike kugu tayi cike da rashin kunya tace " me kake nufi dani khaleed tunda wancar karuwar matarka ta tafi shine ni kake neman kuntatani.

    Toh wallahi ko Hajiya Aisha bata isa ba balle ka..."


 Bata karasa ba yarufeta da mugun duka sosai yake kwallo da ita jibgarta yake Chaiii sai da yaga bata iya ko motse sannan ya kyaleta.
😂

Janta yayi ya watsota waje ya rufe ɗakin wasu zafafan hawaye na zuba a idanuns ay bashiri ya tattara kayansa yabar gidan.


      ****-****
Kallon Hauwa tayi  kafin sake murmushi tace " Mummy na yarda da kaddara me kyau ko marar kyau kibar damuwa wallahi Mummy bantaɓa zina ba tunda Allah ya halicceni akam me zanyi bayan nasan illarsa "

sai ta sunkuyar da kanta a kasa tana share hawaye sannan tacigaba da magana tace " wannan jarabawata ce Ana gwada Imanina ne kuma na yarda da kaddara.

*42....Թɑցҽ*




Wani tausayinta ne ya rufe Hauwa har hawaye na zuba a idanunta rungumeta tayi tana shafa bayanta tace " kiyi hakuri mahakurci mawadaci wata rana sai labari stop cry my dear."


Rarrashinta yayunta suke.


     ****-****
Tunda khaleed yabar kaduna abuja yawucce inda yabar salmah sai da tayi awa biyu tana kwance kafin ta dawo dai dai mikewa tayi ta faɗa ɗakinta tayi wanka da ruwan zafi bayan tafito ne takira Aina'u ta faɗa mata abinda ya faru dariya ta mata inda ta faɗawa Salmah bakar magana da Allah yakara.🤔


Wani kuka salmah tasaka wato ita Aina'u zata yaudara zatayi maganinta.



       Ita Aina'u bakin cikinta da dalilin faɗawa salmah magana abu ɗaya ne dan me zata tunzira khaleed bayan bata cima kudirinta ba.
😳


Da haka sukayi baran baran wannan kenan.

       ****-****
Kwana biyu da zuwa abuja jirginsu ya tashi sai south sudan inda zasuyi aikin a Dafur.

      ****-****
A sokoto kuwa kukari suke su farantawa Ayusha rai ba laifi kume yana tafiya dai dai duk da jarabawar tafito  tasame jami'ar ɗan fodio amma fur taki jami'a daga  karshe school of nursing aka nema mata dake Hauwa tana aiki a makarantar sai kome yazo da sauki....

     Haka rayuwa ta juya mata lkc ɗaya dan ma tana kokarin ɓoye damuwarta a gaban iyayenta amma idan ita da yayunta ne kuka take musu sabida wani mugun Son Khaleed ne yake nukurkusata bata taba sanin halin da take ciki shine so sai da wata yar ajinsu takewa  wata yarinya tambaya Me so ???

       Mafi muni a so shine kaso wanda bai san kana sonshi ba  duk da haka kana dawainiya da sonshi.

       Wasu suna kokarin bawa so hakkinsa. Amma wasu basayin haka.

     Kaɗan daga cikin almomin So shine idan kina son shi da zarran kin ganshi zuciyarki zata shiga jin bugawa jikinki zai ɗauki rawa zakina haba haba domin ki haɗu da shi duk inda kika ganshi zakina satar kallonsa haka kawai kome yayi zakina jin ya burgeki baki gajiya da son jin muryarsa kome yayi kyau yake miki koda kuwa bai da ɗabi'u na gari.

    Akwai wani alama So wanda haka kawai daga haɗuw  farko zaki  kin ji kin tsane shi kome yayi haushinsa kike ji bazai taɓa gwaninta ba awajinki baki kaunar ganinsa koda kuwa yana binki da kalaman so ne amma da zarran zakiji wani abu yasame shi ke zaki fi kowa shiga damuwa.

Akwai wani alama wanda shima So ne amma cike yake da rauni wasu suna amfanin da wannan raunin wajen cutar da Masoyansu.

Haɗawa yayi ya kunna laptop ɗin ya yan taɓe taɓe kafin kome ya ɗauka tar hoton salmah da abinda take aikatawa ya baiyana akan  screen ɗin laptop ɗin duk wani abinda takeyi duk an naɗa shi  kai har bin mazan dake abin ba kyan ganin.

        Fita tayi ta samo guba babu kowa kishiyanta tana ɗaki.
Kitchen tashiga ta zuba a cikin abinci ta fita.

       Babban kuskuren da tayi shine mantawa da tayi yaranta suna gida basu tafi skull ba harsu uku namiji ɗaya mata biyu namijin shine babba sai kannensa mata suna tashi daga bacci kitchen suka shiga ɗibar abincin sukayi dan bakin hali ko ya Matar ubansu basu barwa ba suka xauna abinsu suka cinyen.

         Sun iya tashi suka shiga ɗaki toh daga nan ne dai sukayita fama da rayuwarsu har Allah ya amshe rayuwar babu wanda yasani sai da yar aikin gidan tashiga dubasu tawa karamar wanka tashiga ta gansu kunfa da jini suna fita daga bak'insu ihu ta lafta wanda yasa mai gidan da amaryan suka fito ganin halin da suke ciki yayi yunkurin kaisu asibiti amaryar tace " Baban Umme ko ka kaisu babu abinda za'a musu sabida babu sun tafi."

        Kuka take yayinda wasu daga cikin y'an uwan mijin suka kira yan sanda akayi gaba da ita inda tashiga faɗi faɗe.

       Sabida irin gudun da sukayi sai karfe biyar dai dai suka iso suna shiga ko parking ba'a gama ba ya fita dags motar.


      Kaman zaici da ka ya shiga 
Falon Ummi tana zaune dasu Shuwa sun gaida ita dukkansu Uku da yaransu sai Mufid da Muktar ya kawo shi kwana biyu duk suna zaune a falo suna hirar duk hirar akan auren Ayusha da za'ayi ne.


         "Assalamun Alaikum"
         Sallam da yayi wanda yasa duk suka mike tare da amsa masa da "Amin Wa'alaikumun Salam "

        "Barka da zuwa Ya khaleed  ya hanya "7

        Dukkansu suka haɗa baki suna gaiɗa shi.

    Gaban Ummi yaje ya zube ya ɗaura kansan akan cinyarta yana zubda hawaye a hankali yashiga shesheka ko gizau karshe da ya isheta ture kansa tayi tace " lafiya zaka zo kasani a gaba da kuka lkc da kayi abinka kayi shawara dani ne sai yanzun da ta kwaɓe maka ne zaka zo kana kuka khaleed ɗauka kayi Allah zai barka haka ne ka saki yar mutane har uku."

         Girgiza kai yayi yace " Ummi wallahi ban saketa ba kuma bani da niyyar sakinta kitaimaki rayuwata karna rasata Ummi Aishatu itace rayuwata itace farin cikina don Allah kirufa min asiri kisa baki ta dawo don Allah"







        Kallon shi tayi har cikin kwayar idanunsa gaskiya ta hango haɗi da nadama,

      Kau da kai tayi kafin tace " toh wanca sakin na waye'"
         "na salmah ce ni ga abinda na rubuta anan"


           Mika mata yayi ta buɗe  ga abinda ke ciki=-

      _Amincin Allah ya tabbata a gareka Abokina ga Aishatu nan ta zauna  Awajenka kafin na dawo duk da naso na kawo ta dakaina sai dai tafiyar takamani nasan zata zo cikin wani irin hali wannan yana da nasaba da rashin jituwar da muka samu ne idan ka sami wasikar karka kirani ina cikin wani yanayi ne marar daɗi don Allah_
     Ga abokinka 
       Khaleed Sagir tambuwal,



 Koda ta gama karantawa mikewa tayi tace " har yanzun ban yarda ba ina neman karin hujja kuma bai yarɗa kaje gidan muktar ba ka bashe ya aurar da ita inda zata same nutsuwa ki kaje kuwa sai na saɓa maka "

   tashig ɗakinta cike da farin ciki amma dole ya ladabtar da shi.

        Duk ya birkici ya fita hayacinsa duk a lkc ɗaya.

     Mufid ne yazo ya zauna a jikinsa yana share masa hawaye yace " Auntyns kullum cai tayi kuka "

       Kallon yaron yayi na yan lkc kafin ya juya ys kalle Shuwa yace " ɗan waye a cikinku"

        " Cikin da katafi ka barta dashi ne ta haifa" karama tabashi amsa,


Babu wands yace masa cikanka rungume yaron yayi a jikinsa haɗi da sumbtar yaron yau yaga jininsa a duniya.


     Haka duk suka koma gidajensu cike da tausayin ɗan uwansu.

Kusan sati biyu yana bin Ummi yana bata hakuri amma fir taki sauraransa karshe har kaduna suka zo ganin Salmah sabida asibiti sun dame shi da waya tare suka zo da ita da shuwa ganin.

*46....page*



Dukda taji abinda salmah tayi amma haka baisa ta saurare shi ba.



     Karshe wajen muktar yaje kaman babu kome a tsakaninsu duk kunya ta gama rufe shi dakyar ya faɗa masa yanda kome ya faru har da sake wasikar da salmah tayi.

      Sai da ya bari yakai aya kafin muktar yace " kash frnd kayi hakuri kawai ka sauwake mata domin yanzun haka saura sati biyu ta auri wani kenan kayi hakuri lkc da na baka ita bata san ciwon kanta ba yanzun kuwa tasan me kyau da marar kyau kayi hakuri tunda har akwai wancan sakin."


     Duk wani hakuri yabashi amma fur muktar yaki amincewa.


Dole ya hakura ya tafi.

     ****-****

A cikin sati na uku kana ganinsa sai ka ɗauka mahaukacine sabida yanda ya susuce ya wani koma wani irin ga uban kasunba da yabari a fuskansa.


     Satin da za'a fara bikin ya sake komawa anan ya haɗu da ita ta dawo aiki ai ganin yanda take tafiya cikin farin unifom yafi tafiya da zuciyarsa.


      Suna zaune ne da muktar ta dawo gaidasu tayi ta wucce inda zuciyarta yashiga bugawa ganinsa da tayi ya fama mata ciwon da take ɓoyewa na tsawon shekara uku a ranta.


   Shima ta ɓangarensa kaman ya ɗauketa yake ji musaman da tasamu gyaran da akewa amare.


  

              Hakuri muktar yabashi akan bazai iya cewa ya fasa bawa Dr kabir Ayusha ba mikewa khaleed yayi yace " Mata ce har yanzun kuma na rantse muku amma kuke ganin karya nake ba damuwa"


     Yana gama faɗa haka yafita yabar gidan ji yake duk faɗin sokoto yayi masa zafi duk yarasa meke masa daɗi wazai  samu da maganar shiru yayi ya ɗauke wuta.


        Baban muktar shine ya faɗu masa ay daga nan unguwarsu ya nufa shaf ya manta da malam saleh yana isa gidan ya faka yasame malam a zaure nan suka gaisa.

       Nan ya faɗa masa abinda ke faruwa.
     Shiru malam kafin yace " duk kunyi kuskure musamman kai ay duk abinda  ka gani karkayi saurin yanke hukunci akai koda Abokinka yazo min da labarin na karyata sakin amma rashin zuwanka yasa kome ya lalace dan haka kaje Insha Allah matarka zata dawo gareka ka rage saurin yanke hukunci kai tsaye Allah yasa mu dace"


   godiya yayi sosai sannan yayi masa sallama ya tafi gida.


     Malam saleh da kansa yaje gidan Iyayen dr Kabir ya musu bayani yanda zasu gane  har suka gamsu da bayaninsa.



      Sallama yayi musu daman mahaifiyar Kabir bata son auren dan haka tafi kowa murna faruwan haka.



Daga can gidan Ummi yazo bayan sun gaisa yace " Hajiya Banji daɗin abinda kukayi ba akan me baza ku bashi goyan baya ba ya dawo da matarsa ba sai ma shamakin da kike masa da ita don Allah kiyi hakuri da abinda ya faru"

     haka yayi ta bata hakuri ya hana magana babu yanda tayi tace " shikenan malam ya hucce Allah yabasu zaman lafiya"

     Godiya yayi itama godiya masa sallama yayi mata sannan dawo gida kiran muktar yayi ya faɗa masa yanda sukayi da Khaleed da iyayen Kabir da kuma Ummi dan haka ya maidawa khaleed matarsa,
Shikenan yace kawai.

   ****-****

       An shiga satin biki inda suka fara event dake mijin Maryam gidansu yan halin sunnan ne babu wani bidi'a sosai kamu akayi laraba alhamis akayi fulani day babu wanda yakawo  Khaleed zai zo wajen sai ganinsa akayi yaxo cikin dakakiyar Shadda gezani yayi kyau sosai karku ga yanda yan mata ke binsa da eyes abinda ya bawa Ayusha haushi kenan tabar cikin mutane ta koma gefe tana me jin haushi marar dalili ga Kabir bai zo ba.


     Tana cikin tunani ne yazo wajen kare mata kallo yayi shima take yaji ransa ya ɓaci kama hannunta yayi ya fita da ita ta wani kofa sabida kayan da tasaka kai babu ɗankwalin kirki ga wani fitinanen bukatarta da yake ji.

     Yana fita ya jefata a mota kiran muktar yayi yace " malam surikina na ɗauki mata fa wallahi bazan iya ba tunda bawani auren za'a sake ɗaurawa ba munyi gaba.



      Kashe wayar muktar yayi sannan ya kalle Hauwa yace " ya ɗauki matarsa fa kuma alama tafiya zaiyi da ita domin ɗaxun yazo ya amshe passport ɗinta mu dai muna musu fatan alkhairi."


        Dariya tayi tace " kalan soyayyan Khaleed daban ne da na kowa"

     Haka suka cigaba da tattauwa har yan fulani day suka dawo sai anan ne suka lura babu Ayusha maryam ce ta fara lura da haka nan hankalinsu ya tashi koda suka zo da maganar muktar yace " eh mijinta ya d'auketa sun tafi daman bashi yayi sakin ba "

       Shikenan yaka kashe boss.

   Washe gari juma'a  bayan sallah juma'a aka d'aura auren Maryam da bilal.
   Fatima da Faruq
    Akan sadaki dubu hamsin.


      Da dare aka kai amare gidajensu inda za'a cigaba da shagalin


      ****-****
  Tunda suka dauki hanya take kuka bata bar kuka ba sai da ta gansu airport inda suka jira aka gama kome sannan ya jata suka shiga sai Abuja suna sauka suka jira wani jirgin da zashi Dubai.


    Sai karfe sha daya suka shiga jirgin Dubai wanda basu isa ba sai washe gari da misalin karfe tara.

       Wani motar alfarma ce tazo daukarsu inda taisu  katafaren  hotel anan cikin AbuDhabi.

         Duk sun gaji sallah dake kansu suka biya sannan sukayi wanka suka kwanta basu tashe ba sai karfe biyu inda yunwar cikinsu ya tashesu nan ya dannan wayar da take hadi da kitchen ya fada musu abinda zasu kawo musy har lkc Ayusha fushi take da shi.

    Bayan ankawo abincin suka ci Sallah sukayi nan yashiga rarrashinta tayi hakuri k'in sauraransa tayi karshe cikin hikima da wayyo da dabara ya shawo kanta inda tuni yafara mata abinda za sata dariya tuni suka d'inke abinsu daga hirar dariya sai gashi ya fara d'aura mata Darasinsu na baya.


        A hankali yake binta cike da kewar juna kafin sun ankara tuni sunyi jefa da kayan jikinsu inda suka shiga shayar da junarsu madarar kauna da soyayya zallarta sai ga Khaleed yana kuka itama kuka take sosai.

🙉


     Sun jima suna shan zumar junarsu kafin suka dawo normal dakansa ya mata wanka kaman ya bude kirjinsa ya boyeta haka yakeji.

     Tun daga nan suka bude duniyar love zallah.
  
   Sati guda sukayi a Dubai suka wucce new york  kwanan su biyar daga can suka sauka a birnin london.

     Shima sati sukayi sabida zai kalle kallo anan cikin garin london.


    Daga nan saudiya suka yada zango infa sukayi Umra sannan bayansun gama suka dawo gida.


   Inda suka sauka a sokoto a gidan Ummi.

        Ganinsu tare  ba karamin farin ciki taji aranta abubuwan da aka girki ba'a fadan su nan sukaci suka koshe hirar khaleed  da Ummi suke yayinda Ayusha taje ta fama da tashin zuciya ai kafin tayi wani yunkuri tuni ta fara a falon.

       D'akin Ummi suka shiga ta wanke bakinta da jikinta.

     Zama tayi shiru tana tunani kusan cikin wata biyu kenan a jikinta.
       Tsakanin Khaleed da Ummi an rasa wayafi wani farinciki.


     Kwanasu goma bayansun zaga dangin da aboƙar arziki har da gidajen Amare suka ɗauki ɗansu sai kaɗuna asibitin da salmah take jinya suka fara zuwa har ɗakinta suka je inda tasha ɗauri da sarka kallonta Khaleed yayi yace " Ance min kina gane mutane bansan wani kaddara ya kaini na aureki ba Amma Alhamdullilah ga ɗana da matata ga wani a ciki Insha Allah bazan kara miki kome ba sabida yanzun ma yakika kare da hakkin jama'a sakine dai na dunkula namiki kaman yanda kika min uku "

        ihu take shi kuwa juyawa yayi da Ayusha d mufid.

         Suna fitowa wannan gurgun ya taresu yana kuka su yafe masa khaleed yace " jeka mun yafe maka"


     Daga nan gida suka koma inda yasha gyara kome na gidan sabo ne.

      Bayan tayi abinci sunci yace " gobe zamu bar gidan nan zamu koma Abuja dan haka karki buɗe kome "

   " Toh "kawai tace masa,

   Da dare k'in barinta yayi ta huta sai da ya lashe zumarsa.


       ****-****
  Washi gari suka tashi daga kaduna sai  Abuja.


     Da misalin karfe ɗaya suka isa Sani Abacha barrack.


   Sun same gidan a gyare haɗi da kome da kome


      Sai dasuka huta sannan suka shiga duba gidan.

   Ba laifi yayi.
 Rayuwarsu gwanin daɗi suke bayan watani ta haifi yarta mace ranar suna yarinya taci sunan Hauwa amma suna kiranta ɗa Hanifa.


       ****-****

   Bayan shekara goma zaune take tana sharyar da yaronta me wata huɗu gefe Mufid ne da kannensa yana musu home worka.

     Ɓbayan Hanifa tasake haihuwar maza sai wanda take shayar dashi takwar Khaleed ta haifi me sunan Muktar sai  takwaran malam saleh.



     Shiru yayi yace " Y'an mata zan kara aure fa yar yarinyar nake so"

      Ɗdariya tayi kafin  tace " kayi mana ai ni bana tsoran kishiiyata jahadina ce ko kaga Salmah ta bani tsoro ne saima nice na zame mata barazana Allah yajinkanta banson tayi mumunan karshe ba duk wacce zata zo sauran Ayusha zata samu ko "

    Murmushi yayi yace " haka ne da mata zasu gane da basu ɗauki kishiya abokiyar gaba ba domin kunsan taimakon juna ne in baki da lafiya inkin haihu itace zata cigaba da kula  da mijin da yaran da gidan kome "

    Dariya tayi tace " gaskiya ne maganarka"


 Haka suka cigaba da hirar cike da farin ciki.
